### Item 1: the timeline measured (mission `msn_ef86659ab11d50f4dfee`, test account, completed; all UTC, 4 Nov 2026)
| Time | Event | Gap | Cause |
|---|---|---|---|
| 12:19:32.4 | created (queued) | | |
| 12:19:35.0 | worker «بدأ الوكيل دراسة طلبك» | 2.6 s | worker poll |
| 12:19:36.0 | question «من أنهي منصة؟» | 1.0 s | planning |
| 12:19:55.8 | answer «إكس» saved | 19.8 s | the customer reading and tapping |
| **12:20:45.0** | **worker picks it up** | **49.2 s** | **NOT explained.** The worker polls every 5 s, so the poll is not the cause. Leading suspect: `memoryBlocksAdmission` (admit limit 300 MB, `AGENT_MEMORY_ADMIT_LIMIT_MB`; the planning run had started at 297 MB tree RSS and idle readings are 242 to 268 MB); it logs `memory_wait` and takes nothing while blocked. Not provable from the database. |
| 12:20:46.5 | count question «تحب كام تغريدة؟» | 1.5 s | planning |
| 12:21:21.0 | count answer «١٠» | 34.5 s | the customer |
| 12:21:25.2 | worker picks it up | 4.2 s | worker poll (normal) |
| 12:21:29.0 | plan stored (10 rows, reserve 200) | 3.8 s | planning |
| 12:23:14.2 | approval | 105 s | the customer |
| 12:23:16.2 | execution starts | 2.0 s | worker |
| 12:23:19.1 | completed (10 delivered, charged 200) | 2.9 s | execution |
Page side: the card asked every 8 s when the mission was not live (question and plan states), so a new card could show up to 8 s late; that is what the faster polling addresses. **Heroku logs are not readable from the sandbox.** To prove or drop the memory hypothesis, paste the Heroku log lines `memory_wait`, `memory_ok` and `claim_failed` for 12:19 to 12:21 UTC. The worker heartbeat history is in Storage (not read, by rule).

### Item 4: which missions were counted
Read-only SELECTs of missions active between 12:19 and 12:24 UTC returned **only `msn_ef86659ab11d50f4dfee` itself** (no other mission of any customer was queued, approved, planning or executing then). So `readQueue`/`queueLine` could not have produced a queue line from other missions. The words «في الدور» are the state label of a QUEUED mission (`STATE_AR.queued` in `openMissions.ts`, «في الدور وتبدأ تلقائيًا عند دورها»), and the mission was `queued` from 12:19:55 to 12:20:45 (the 49 s gap). The label was true of the mission and misleading to read; it now says what is true (above). Not changed: `readQueue` counts `running` without excluding the mission itself; that does not matter on the queued path (the line is only drawn for queued or approved) but is worth a `neq("id", ...)` later.

### Item 7: what the probe showed (nothing changed in the PDF code yet)
The methodology PDF is drawn by `src/lib/agentMission/methodology/pdf.ts` (pdfkit, the IBM Plex Sans Arabic font, text passed through `formatArabicRtl`, which reshapes and reverses, so the content stream holds presentation-form glyphs). A probe (outside the repo) drew the words of the owner's list with the same font and `formatArabicRtl` and ran the real `pdftotext` (poppler, at `/usr/bin/pdftotext` in this container): the extracted words are wrong, e.g. «في» → «يف», «الفترة» → «الفرتة», «الإكسل» → «اإلكسل», «لا فترة» → «فرتة ال» (letters swapped or in visual order; the owner's copy-paste lost letters). Drawing the LOGICAL string (no `formatArabicRtl`) with pdfkit also extracts wrongly («يف», «الفرتة»), so "draw logical text and let the font shape it" is NOT a fix by itself. It is a font and ToUnicode problem, not a one-line fix.

### Next steps (after this round)
**Item 7 (its own round).** (a) Red test first: build the methodology PDF with `renderMethodologyPdf` from a stored document, run `pdftotext -raw` (or pdf.js) on the bytes, and require every Arabic word of `documentLines(document)` to appear exactly. (b) The probe above (ActualText per line) restored words but reversed mixed Arabic-Indic digits; try an invisible logical-text overlay per line (render mode 3), then a post-processed ToUnicode CMap. (c) Do not touch the price or the charge path (`chg_meth_<jobId>`). (d) Keep `tests/helpers/pdfText.ts` tests green.
**Item 1.** Paste the Heroku log lines `memory_wait`, `memory_ok` and `claim_failed` for 12:19 to 12:21 UTC on 4 Nov. If `memory_wait` shows, decide the admission policy for the no-model planning step (a separate, careful change; do not weaken the memory guard blindly).
**Item 9.** A real signed-in before/after timing on a preview build (the sandbox has no customer session). Look at the request waterfall in the browser: session read, `/api/wallet/balance`, and what it waits for.
**Item 8 (owner decision).** The count in the title of a request «with all comments and replies»: today the count of the whole file with the noun of the last stage. If the owner wants the parent noun and count (e.g. «فيديوهان عن ...»), say so.

## Round 25 (4 Nov 2026, after #375, main = `42d58e7`): the platform answer survives the count answer; the legacy model path takes only companies and places

**No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call, no key or token seen.** No forbidden file edited (the `runMission.ts` changes are planning routing, not settlement).

**1. Live mission `msn_04afab34eab34d815c6b`** (test account; read-only SELECTs on missions, mission_events, mission_calls): 11:51:30 created «عايز داتا عن رأي الناس في الذكاء الاصطناعي»; 11:51:36 card «من أنهي منصة؟»; 11:51:41 answer «إكس»; 11:51:46 the compiled X path asked the count before any read («تحب كام تغريدة؟…», ١٠ / ٥٠ / ١٠٠ / ميزانية); 11:53:00 answer «١٠»: `answer_text` became «١٠» and the platform answer was REPLACED (every answer replaces `answer_text`); 11:53:01 the request alone named no platform, the round-23 question was skipped because `answer_text` was not empty, and the LEGACY model path ran: one paid search `anyapi.x.search.posts` («الذكاء الاصطناعي lang:ar -is:retweet», 20 rows, $0.00054) and $0.0067 of model, no card → «unsupported» → unserved «مصادرنا لا تخدم» at 11:53:27. No plan was ever priced (plan null, reserved null): the «plan card» was the card's live «خطة المهمة» panel during those 26 seconds. The count card WAS answered («١٠»).
Fix: the platform tapped at «من أنهي منصة؟» is kept in the run note (`platformAnswer`, like the occurrence and the count choices) and the request is read with «على <platform>» for the whole mission (`withPlatformChoice`). The Ask topic of «رأي الناس في X», «آراء الجمهور عن X», «كلام الناس حول X» is X (`requestContract.topicOf`). A planning run that ends with no card never closes a mission that already has a plan.
**2. The legacy model-planned path takes only companies or places** (`legacyRouteOf`). No platform + social-like → the platform card; anything else unclear (and Reddit, LinkedIn and other platforms the compiled path does not serve) → ONE clarifying card (five platforms + «قائمة شركات أو أماكن»); a second unclear answer ends with `UNCLEAR_END_AR`, no paid call, never «مصادرنا لا تخدم». All before any model turn or provider call. Skipped under the test seam `modelPlanningForTests()`, like the social gate.
Tests: `tests/round25-platform-answer.test.ts` (the live mission replayed through `runPlanning` to a compiled X plan of ten tweets; 30 vague requests with no model run and no provider call; companies and places still legacy). **Not tested:** the live site; Reddit and LinkedIn requests now get the clarifying card instead of the legacy path.

## Round 23 (4 Nov 2026, after #373, main = `4aae39a`): six live findings of the 30-scenario test (platform question, privacy, open-missions limit, trust, account questions, greeting name)

**No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call, no key or token seen.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is edited. Item 6 needs `chatHandler.ts` (forbidden): the helper is built and tested, the handler's change is reported, not made.

**1. A vague request is asked, not refused.** Diagnosis (read-only SELECT on `missions`, `mission_events`, `mission_calls` of `msn_bcb47217…`): «عايز داتا عن رأي الناس في الذكاء الاصطناعي» names no platform, so the compiled path (social platforms only) did not take it; the model-planned path ran 20 model turns (planning LLM about $0.0096), made TWO paid searches on X (the model guessed the platform), then ended with no card: class «unsupported» → status `unserved`, «مصادرنا لا تخدم هذا الطلب…». `platformQuestion.ts` + `runMission.ts`: a request that asks for what people say (opinions, comments, posts, videos, reactions) with no platform, no answer yet and no other kind of source (companies, maps, contacts, sites, files) becomes `needs_input` with the question «من أنهي منصة؟» and five choices (تيك توك، إكس، إنستجرام، فيسبوك، يوتيوب) BEFORE any model turn or provider call; the answer is read with the request, so its platform takes the normal compiled path. The card showed at most four choices (`customerView.ts`), now six. **The unserved e-mail** is sent by `sendMissionNotice` (`src/lib/server/missionNotify.ts`, called by the worker's `tell()` in `agent/src/runMission.ts` for kind `unserved`, template «job-update» through `sendPlatformEmail`; the text is `notices.ts`): not in `src/functions/**`. An `unserved` notice with no job (declined before anything started) now writes the in-site notice only. A run that had a job keeps its e-mail (the one-minute wait rule lives in `src/functions/**` and was not touched).
**2. Privacy** (`guardRules.ts`, checked by code BEFORE the model): phone or WhatsApp numbers, home addresses and personal e-mails of private individuals are refused with one sentence on privacy and an offer (public posts and comments; business contacts of companies and decision makers, which stay allowed). Explicit private words («ناس عادية», «المعلقين», «المتابعين», «أصحاب الحسابات», «عناوين سكن») refuse even beside a business word.
**3. The open-missions limit** applies only to a NEW data request (it is raised only by `createCustomerMission`, after the type is known). The live replies came from misclassification (the keyword fallback read «ممكن أغير اسمي في الحساب؟» as a data request: «ممكن» + «حساب»; the privacy ask was a data request too). Fixed by the pre-model rules and tested with three open missions.
**4. Trust** (`guardRules.ts`): a fixed honest answer (nothing before approval, the plan and price seen first, charge only for what is delivered, an undelivered hold returns whole). Why the model path fell back: the production log showed `chat_message_type … source: keyword, type: other` for that turn (the model's answer was not used; the reply was the keyword «لم أفهم»); the failure code was NOT logged, so the exact cause (http error, timeout, unreadable reply) cannot be proven from logs (Hobby keeps one hour). Two likely causes were fixed: a JSON reply with a raw line break inside its text was unreadable (now mended) and a reply cut at the token limit lost its type (now read from the first field). The log line now carries `failure`.
**5. Account questions** (`guardRules.ts`): answers name only places that exist (a test reads their source): the name in «الإعدادات» → «الملف الشخصي» → «الاسم» → «حفظ»; the password in «الإعدادات» → «كلمة المرور» (and «نسيت كلمة المرور؟» on the login page); delete in «الإعدادات» → «حذف الحساب»; top-up in «المحفظة» → «شحن الرصيد». There is NO e-mail field in the settings: the answer says it is not available yet and points to «تواصل معنا».
**6. Greeting.** `src/lib/greetingName.ts` (`arabicDisplayName`, `balanceLineAr`). The sentence is built in `src/functions/shared/chatHandler.ts` (~line 2105-2135, forbidden): the smallest change is reported in the PR.
Tests: `tests/round23-live-fixes.test.ts`, `ROUND23_LIVE` in `tests/helpers/chatCorpus.ts`. **Not tested:** the real model; the live site; the page's rendering of five choices in a browser.

## Round 22 (4 Nov 2026, after #372, main = `62959fb`): the chat talks like an assistant (the chat model names the type and writes the reply, code is the guard)

**No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call, no key or token seen.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is edited (`chatHandler.ts` is untouched: everything is inside `agentChatTurn`; the balance is READ with `getBalance`).

**Why.** Live after #372 the round-21 keyword classifier made the chat robotic: «يعني ايه ديجر الذكي» got «لم أفهم من رسالتك أنها طلب لجمع بيانات», and a terse follow-up on an open plan («خليها ٥ تعليقات بس») got the same short reply instead of editing the plan.

**What.**
 - `src/lib/agentMission/chatModel.ts`: ONE request per message to the chat model (the Anthropic key the product already uses, through `resolveProviderKey`; `max_tokens` 400, thinking off, 12 s timeout; a failure is a value, never thrown). Under a test runner the production call refuses to run unless a test injects a fake (`setChatModelForTesting`): a test can never make a paid call.
 - `src/lib/agentMission/conversationModel.ts`: `buildSystemPrompt` (who Diger is, the five platforms and companies, the fixed facts from code: minimum 200 points, 1 point = ٠٫١٠ جنيه, nothing before approval, only what is delivered, the methodology PDF 50 points, the balance, the open request, the strict rules), `parseModelAnswer` (one line of JSON: type, reason, reply, count), `guardReply` (blocks provider names, endpoint and internal ids, dollars, Latin words and digits, emoji, model names and makers, «صف»/«سجل», and any number that is not a fact of the code, the balance or a number the customer wrote; a blocked reply is replaced by the round-21 fixed reply of its type) and `decideTurn`.
 - Types: `greeting_or_small_talk`, `service_question`, `methodology_request`, `data_request`, `edit_of_open_request`, `other`, validated against that set. WHEN UNSURE THE ANSWER IS `data_request`: a message the keyword rules read as a clear request is never refused, whatever the model said. `edit_of_open_request` exists only with an open request (planning, a question card, a plan waiting); without one it is a data request. A failed call, an unreadable reply or an unknown type falls back to the round-21 keyword rules (plus the short edit or answer of an open request, by code).
 - `chatTurn.ts`: the model names the type; only a data request reaches planning (as before). An edit goes to the open request's OWN path, applied by the code that already does it: a plan waiting for approval gets `editPlanForCustomer({count})` (priced again by code; the count must be a number the customer wrote, the model cannot invent one), a question card gets `answerForCustomer` (the mission is queued again), a plan still being prepared gets a fixed sentence; a follow-up with no count points to the card's «عدّل الطلب». The chat's own retry and review button messages are data requests by code and cost no model call. A methodology request keeps round 21: the model writes the sentences about what the document is, the code adds the chooser sentence and the price line (50 points) or the «ليس لديك طلب مُسلَّم» offer, and a clear ask for a FULL research methodology still gets the honest sentence.
 - Tests: `tests/round22-chat-model.test.ts` (corpus of 79 messages in `tests/helpers/chatCorpus.ts`: the round-21 corpus, the live messages, follow-ups with an open request, ambiguous data requests; a fake model in the real format, `tests/helpers/fakeChatModel.ts`; guard, prompt, routing, edit/answer paths, transport with a faked network).
**Not tested:** the real chat model (no paid call is allowed): its actual wording, the type it gives to unusual messages and its latency were not observed; nothing was run on the live site.

## Round 21 (4 Nov 2026, after #368, main = `618ca84`): the message type before planning, the card's first sentence, the data-collection methodology (PDF, 50 points)

**No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call, no key or token seen.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is edited (`chatHandler.ts`, `fileGenerators.ts`, `facebookReportFiles.ts` and the RPC `purchase_methodology_atomic` are only READ or IMPORTED). The charge goes through the existing route, RPC and ledger id `chg_meth_<jobId>`; the only change to it is the price constant (10 → 50, owner-approved).

**A. Findings (confirmed in the code).**
 - A: `AGENT_SERVES_CHAT = true` (`src/lib/agentMission/constants.ts:18`) and the handler's branch `if (this.agentChat?.enabled && sb && ... && !isGreeting && !isGeneralQuestion)` (`src/functions/shared/chatHandler.ts:2711`) hands EVERY message the handler's own greeting list does not know to `agentChatTurn` (`src/lib/agentMission/chatTurn.ts`), which created a mission for it. «هاي يا صاحبي» is not in that list; «عايزك تعملي منهجية بحث تكون pdf» reached the planner and ended «مصادرنا لا تخدم هذا الطلب».
 - B: `PLANNING_USUAL_TIME_AR` (`src/lib/missionView.ts:54`) was only «ولن يُخصم شيء قبل موافقتك.»: round 14 removed «عادةً دقيقة أو اتنين،» and left the tail, and the waiting card shows it first.
 - C: the methodology feature: `ResultsCanvas.tsx:554,583` (the button), `src/app/api/jobs/[id]/methodology/route.ts` (`METHODOLOGY_DEFAULT_POINTS`, `generatePdfReport`; it reads `data.json`, which a mission never has), `methodologyChargeId` (`src/lib/methodologyOffer.ts`), `methodologyPurchase.ts`, the RPC `purchase_methodology_atomic` (takes any positive amount: no SQL change was needed for 50).

**1. The message type, by code, before planning** (`src/lib/agentMission/messageType.ts`, called from `agentChatTurn`, so `chatHandler.ts` is not touched): one of `greeting`, `service_question`, `methodology_request`, `data_request`, `other`. Only `data_request` creates a mission; every other type is answered from fixed words and facts of the code (minimum 200 points = `DEFAULT_MISSION_PRICING_SETTINGS`, 1 point = ٠٫١٠ جنيه, nothing before approval, only what is delivered, the methodology 50 points = `METHODOLOGY_POINTS`), writes the answer to the assistant message and ends the customer's message `completed`: no mission row, no model, no provider call (the test spies on `fetch`). The classifier is a pure function (no model: the owner allowed one, but it was not needed and its output would have to be validated back to the same fixed set). A message that carries a request is the request (greeting + request = data); a price question about the methodology is a service question. The permanent corpus is `tests/round21-message-type.test.ts` (54 Arabic and English messages, Egyptian and formal, the two live ones first, every type covered, plus the chat buttons' own messages; add a mistyped live message there with its expected type).
 - Methodology request: with delivered compiled missions the answer carries `METHODOLOGY_CHOOSER_SENTENCE_AR` and the price line «منهجية الجمع لهذا الطلب: ٥٠ نقطة», and the chat page draws `MethodologyChooser` under it (it recognises that sentence in the message, live and after a reload); with none, the answer says what the methodology is and offers to start a data request; an ask for a FULL research methodology (study design, sample, analysis words) starts with one honest sentence («تكتب ديجر حاليًا منهجية جمع البيانات لطلباتك عبرها، وليس منهجية بحث كاملة...»).
 - Known limit: a terse follow-up with no noun of the service («خليها ٥٠٠») is now «other» and gets the short honest reply instead of a new mission.
**Found by the full suite (fixed):** the chat's own button messages («أعد المحاولة بنفس الخطة», «عايز أراجع الخطة...») were classified «other» and the retry turn stopped creating a quote; `classifyMessage` now sends `RETRY_SAME_PLAN_MESSAGE` and `REVIEW_PLAN_MESSAGE` to the data flow (both are in the corpus). Tests changed with a CHANGED note: `ui-pass-1`, `round14-queue-notice` (the whole sentence), `audit-phase1-flows`, `methodology-offer`, `methodology-wallet-and-offer` (the price 50, written «٥٠ نقطة»: the plural form for 11 and more, also fixed in `methodologyOffer.ts` and `honestDisplay.ts`), `methodology-section` (the guard of workbook readers lists the new read-only reader), `stuck-open-missions-replay` (its placeholder «طلب رابع» is a real data request now).
**2. Finding B fixed:** the sentence is whole again: «نقرأ طلبك ونجهّز لك الخطة والسعر، ولن يُخصم شيء قبل موافقتك.» (no time). `tests/round21-card-sentence.test.tsx` renders the queued and planning cards and reads their first visible text (red before: it started with «ولن»).
**3. The data-collection methodology (PDF) for a delivered compiled mission.**
 - Source: the «المنهجية» section the worker already writes, by code, below the summary of the first sheet of every delivered workbook (`agent/src/methodology.ts`), read back from the delivered Excel (`methodology/readWorkbook.ts`) plus the file's own sheets (delivered counts, columns, the «دقة التاريخ» marks). Nothing is recomputed, estimated or added; no model rewrites a word. `methodology/document.ts` builds the sections (the fixed sentences carry no digit; the document has no date, no page number and no count of its own); `methodology/pdf.ts` draws it with the embedded IBM Plex Sans Arabic font, right to left (visual word order, a number reversed as a whole so it reads in its own order, quotation marks swapped), outside `src/functions` (it only IMPORTS `formatArabicRtl` and the font readers of `fileGenerators.ts`, as `facebookReportPdf.ts` does; `generatePdfReport` reads a `data.json` and was not used).
 - Eligible: `methodology/missions.ts`: status `completed`, `delivered_units > 0`, a job, `plan_public.compiled === true`, an Excel file. Offered on the mission card (`AgentMissionCard`, from the `methodology` field of `GET /api/missions/<id>`), in the chat's chooser (`GET /api/missions/methodology`) and, for old jobs, by the old button.
 - Charge: the EXISTING route `/api/jobs/<jobId>/methodology` (a mission branch before the saved-data path) and ledger id `chg_meth_<jobId>`; 50 points (`METHODOLOGY_DEFAULT_POINTS` and `METHODOLOGY_POINTS`, pinned equal by a test); the file is built and saved BEFORE the charge, a failed read of the workbook, a file with no section or a failed render charges nothing; one charge per mission (the ledger row and the RPC are idempotent), re-download free. **New guard:** the request must name `expectedPoints: 50`; any other (an old tab still showing ١٠) is refused 409 «تغيّر سعر المنهجية» before anything is read or charged, so the charge is never above what the page showed.
 - Download: `GET /api/jobs/<jobId>/methodology?file=1` serves the bought PDF itself under its Arabic name (`Content-Disposition` with `filename*`, PR #368's `fileResponseFromUrl`): «منهجية جمع البيانات - <ما جُمع>.pdf». The old button and the chooser use it.
 - Tests: `tests/round21-methodology-pdf.test.ts` replays the five stored live missions (TikTok M12, YouTube ×2, Facebook ×2), builds the delivered workbook, writes the PDF from it and reads the PDF's own text back out of its bytes (`tests/helpers/pdfText.ts`: ToUnicode maps, drawn order): every number in the PDF is a number of the delivered file and every number of the file's methodology is in the PDF; the letters and digits drawn are exactly the document's; the Arabic is right to left (the title comes out in visual order) and shaped (several glyphs per letter); no provider, endpoint, dollar or vendor; no Western digit outside a link. Mutation check: a foreign number put into a fixed sentence fails it (red), restored (green). `tests/round21-methodology-route.test.ts` (route, fake ledger and storage) and `tests/round21-methodology-ui.test.tsx` (what the customer sees).
**4.** `.gitignore` now ignores `__pycache__/` and `*.pyc` (`tests/round21-gitignore.test.ts`).
**Not tested:** the PDF was not opened in a browser or a PDF viewer by a person (it was rendered to an image with poppler and read by the agent); nothing was run on the live site; the price of the live ledger and the real RPC were not exercised (a fake behaves as the RPC does); a real delivered mission's workbook was not read from Storage (rules); Vercel's response-size limit for the streamed PDF was not checked (the file is small).

**Evidence:** the owner's admin ZIP of `msn_934781c56a01a96825af` (Facebook smoke, test account): 11 files (manifest, 9 raw pages `call_0001..0003`, `0005..0010`, `rows/posts.json`, `rows/comments.json`; `call_0004` was refused live, so it has no page). The call log is read by SELECT (`mission_calls`: id, phase, endpoint_id, status, http_status, rows_returned, cost_usd, params_digest, created_at; `missions`: status, created_at, reserved and unit points) and added to `call-index.json` (a post link is its numeric surrogate; the page listing is indexed by the page's numeric id, so its replay compares the cursor only).
**1. `anyapi.facebook.post_comments` is now VERIFIED, for every account.** What the real pages show (two posts, each read by the main card `scrapecreators.x.v1-facebook-post-comments` and then by the alternative: calls 7/8 and 9/10):
 - list path `output.data.comments`, next-page field `output.data.nextCursor`, end of list `output.data.hasNextPage` (false on both pages). **The alternative gives a non-empty `nextCursor` on a last page** (`cur_...`), exactly as the main card does (`cursor` non-empty with `has_next_page` false): a cursor alone never says there is more; the gateway reads `hasNextPage`, which is why the engine made one call per post per route.
 - rows per page: 4 and 4 (the posts had no more readable comments). **Not measured: a second page, the real page size, the cursor's behaviour.** The card keeps `observedRowsPerPage: 10` (the catalog's) and its price is still the catalog's 0.002 USD (so the main card, 0.00188, stays cheaper and stays the main card: tests pin it).
 - cost per call: 0.0012 USD for 4 rows (the page file's own `costUsd`; the catalog says 0.002, and the planning calls of `anyapi.facebook.user.posts` were also billed 0.0012); the main card: 1 credit, 0.00188 USD a call.
 - ids PROVEN equal: the 8 comments of the two posts have the same id on both cards, in the same order, with the same text, author, instant, reaction count and reply count (`tests/round20-facebook-alt-comments.test.ts` recomputes it from the stored pages; the id decodes to `comment:<post>_<comment>` on both, so the parent post is inside the id). The proof is in `COMMENT_ID_PROOFS` (the alternatives rule `ids_unproven` is satisfied); it is 8 comments on 2 posts, which is more than Instagram's one comment, still thin.
 - parent link: the alternative's rows carry no post field; the parent is the call's own `url` input (the card's `parent_binding`), and the id repeats the post id.
 - **What differs (found by the red test):** the alternative writes a comment's reaction count as `reactions` (a number); the shared replies mapping reads `likes`, `like_count`, `digg_count`... so the likes column was EMPTY for rows from the alternative (the main card's `reaction_count` was read only when a card carries tokens). Fixed for this card only: `mapping.likesFrom: "reactions"` (new optional field of the card) adds a post-pass over the same page; every other card's mapping code is byte for byte what it was.
 - **What it cannot do:** it carries no `feedback_id` or `expansion_token`, so it cannot feed a replies step (the alternatives rule leaves it out when replies are planned: `replies_identity`), unchanged.
 - Effect: a customer's Facebook comments-only stage now plans the alternative (no longer a test-account capture, so no 0.20 USD page cap), the card says the existing «نحاول الإكمال من مصدر آخر للمنصة نفسها» line, and the delivered file's limits say, per post: «المتوقع ٩ تعليقًا، وسُلّم ٤ تعليقًا فريدًا، والفرق ٥؛ جرّبنا المسار الرئيسي ومسارًا بديلًا واحدًا للمنصة نفسها، ولم يعطِ أيٌّ منها الباقي.» (one line per post). **Both routes ended at the same 4 comments of 9 and 6: the alternative added nothing on these two posts; the counters (9, 6) include comments neither route returns.** The mission is added to the delivery-contract replay (`tests/helpers/round14Missions.ts`, `FB20`): zero violations, for a customer and a test account.
**2. The download name.** ROOT CAUSE (read in the code, not on a live response): every file of a mission and of an older request was handed to the browser as a Supabase signed link made with `createSignedUrl(path, ttl, { download: <Arabic name> })`; the storage service writes that name percent-encoded into a plain `filename=` (no `filename*`), and the browser saved «%D8%AA...xlsx» (the mission page opened the bare link; the two job pages fell back to it when their cross-origin read of the link failed). A header the site does not write cannot be fixed there, so the site now serves the file itself: `src/lib/server/fileResponse.ts` reads the signed link on the server and answers with its own header, `src/lib/downloadName.ts` (`contentDispositionOf`): `attachment; filename="<ASCII fallback>"; filename*=UTF-8''<percent-encoded name>` (a name with no ASCII letter falls back to `diger-results.<ext>`; quotes, CR, LF, NUL and slashes are removed; `' ( ) *` are escaped; the storage service's own header is never forwarded). Routes with the new `&stream=1` mode (the JSON mode is unchanged): `/api/missions/[id]/file` (customer), `/api/admin/agent/[id]/file`, `/api/jobs/[id]/download`; `customerFileLink` and `fileLink` also return the `download` name. Pages changed to use it: the mission card (`AgentMissionCard`), «طلباتي», the chat's download, and the admin mission page; `adminDownload` reads `filename*` first. The three CSV and receipt routes that set a header by hand (`calls-log`, `users` CSV, receipts) use the same function. **Not changed:** the zip exports (ASCII names, anchored tests), and the methodology PDF (`/api/jobs/[id]/methodology`: the methodology feature is out of this PR; its signed link still carries `download`). **Not tested live:** a response from Vercel with a large file (the body is streamed, not buffered; Vercel's response size limit for streamed bodies was not checked).
**Tests.** New: `tests/round20-facebook-alt-comments.test.ts` (25), `tests/round20-download-filename.test.ts` (20). RED RUNS first (recorded): the card file failed 6 of 23 on the code of #367 (status, proof, alternative, and the likes column); the download file failed to load, then, with only the two pure modules, 4 of 20 failed on the routes (no `Content-Disposition` on any of them). CHANGED existing tests (each marked in the file): `tests/platform-cards.test.ts` (14 verified, 4 catalog_only), `tests/round16-alternatives-unit.test.ts` and `tests/round16-alternatives-plan.test.ts` (Facebook's second card is an alternative for every account; the capture rule is checked on a synthetic card), `tests/fixtures-anonymised.test.ts` (inventory), `tests/round14-contract-replay.test.ts` (the smoke's refused listing page may stay unserved), `tests/admin-calls-log.test.ts` (`filename*`).

**No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched (the price is READ through the one pricing path; only the number of rows a Facebook-style comments stage plans changed, which lowers a hold, never raises it).

**1. Why no test caught the TikTok failure, and what guards it now.** The tests that planned a mission either ran the compiler alone (`planByCompiler`, no store write) or ran `runPlanning` over the in-memory store (accepts any body), on anonymised pages (every text a placeholder: no half-emoji ever reached a write), with the test seam on. `tests/round19-planning-replay-e2e.test.ts` (40 tests) runs ONE chain per platform: the production `runPlanning` (test seam OFF, the real gateway and caps, the real store writer into a REST stand-in that refuses what the real one refuses, `tests/helpers/fakeRestClient.ts`) over the stored live pages (`tests/helpers/productionPlanning.ts`), the plan AS SAVED, the engine on the stored execution pages, the real workbook, and the delivery contract at ZERO violations; each platform twice (pages as stored; and every anonymised text ending in half an emoji). Cases: TikTok M12, X M14 (the smoke's request; the one stored X page answers the compiled query, stated in the test), Instagram M16, Facebook round 12, YouTube round 11. RED on the store of `df6e730`: the five half-emoji runs fail with `Error: mission update: Empty or invalid json` (every platform shared the vulnerability, not only TikTok); green with #366.
**2. Facebook promise and one line per parent (`msn_934781c56a01a96825af`: counters 9 and 6, ١٠ asked per post).** The comments stage planned `per parent x parents` (20) although its promise type was already the sum of min(asked, counter): the card said ٢٢. Now `compilePlan.ts` plans the promise (9 + 6 = 15, 2 + 15 = 17) when every counter is known; an OPEN stage (no counter before reading) still plans the number asked for every parent. «حدود هذا التسليم»: the line the stage's own rule wrote for a parent is REPLACED by the sentence that also says the routes tried (`alternativesGapBodyAr`, `threadCounters.ts`), never followed by a second one; a parent whose counter is below the number asked says «المتوقع ٩ تعليقًا بحسب عدّاد المنصة». **R1** (delivery contract) now fails a promise above the sum of min(asked, counter) (or above the counters for a stage sized by an edited total); **R2** fails two lines for one parent and accepts a count written in words («تعليق واحد», «تعليقان»: the formatter writes one and two in words, so R2 used to find no digit and ADD a second line: this is what the YouTube smoke's «علامة داخلية: ملف صُحّح وصفه» was). Also found by the Instagram replay: a counter that counts comments only (Instagram, Facebook, YouTube) was made whole by the replies delivered (`c + r`), so 8 comments and 1 reply against a counter of 9 said nothing; only TikTok's counter counts comments and replies (`counterIncludesReplies`). CHANGED existing test: `tests/round14-contract-unit.test.ts` (the R2 case asserted the duplicate completion).
**3. Numbers in words and bare counts (`ask.ts`, `requestContract.ts`, `arabicNumberWords.ts`, `countChoice.ts`).** «فيديو واحد», «منشور واحد», «تغريدة واحدة», the duals, «تلات/ثلاث/خمس/عشر/عشرين/مية», Arabic-Indic and Latin digits are counts. A bare «مع ١٠ تعليقات» with ONE parent is that parent's number (the YouTube smoke asked both questions); with several parents it stays a bare count (`Ask.commentsBare`) and the per-parent question is asked once; «في المجموع» / «إجمالي» is the total (`edits.comments`), never asked. `tests/fixtures/ask-requests.json` (46 requests, 15 live) with the expected Ask and question, `tests/round19-ask-corpus.test.ts` (red on `009d899`: 47 of 50 failed, the new field and the word forms do not exist there).
**4. Capture evidence.** The Facebook smoke mission `msn_934781c56a01a96825af` is the FIRST run of the test-account alternative comments card (`anyapi.facebook.post_comments`): its execution made 2 calls to it (call ids from `msn_934781c56a01a96825af:call_0008`), both ok, 8 rows in all, after the main tool (`scrapecreators.x.v1-facebook-post-comments`, 2 calls, 8 rows). The owner attaches the admin ZIP so its pages can be anonymised (`tests/fixtures/live/anonymise.py`) and the card verified; nothing of that mission is committed.
**Not proven.** (1) The TikTok root cause is inferred from the refused body, not read from the page text (Storage not read): the worker now logs the key path when it repairs text. (2) The stored X page is borrowed for the compiled query (no stored page of the compiled X query exists; the smoke's X mission pages are not in the fixtures). ## Round 19 (4 Nov 2026, after #365): TikTok «تعذّر استكمال مهمتك» (the plan could not be saved), PR A

Base `main` = `df6e730` (#365). **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched. Every diagnosis query was a read-only SELECT (mission rows, `mission_events`, `mission_calls`) or a read-only query on the project's API log; Storage was not read.

**The failure (`msn_e4e2efab054c8ff6669d`, test account, 4 Nov 07:00 UTC).** Request «هات ٢ فيديو تيك توك من مصر بالعربي عن الذكاء الاصطناعي، مع ١٠ تعليقات من كل فيديو». Events: the planning read 4 pages (two searches, two comments pages), the compiler built the plan («خطة مبنية بالكود», `compiled:1`), then «تعثّرت الخطوة الأخيرة مؤقتًا» (re-queued) twice, then «تعذّر على الوكيل إعداد خطة»; `missions.error` = `mission update: Empty or invalid json`, reserve and charge null. The API log: three `PATCH /rest/v1/missions` of **19,463 bytes each, all 400**, at 07:00:44, :49 and :53 (the plan write, `runMission.ts` `updateMission(... status: "plan_ready", plan, plan_public ...)`); in the same smoke the plan writes of X, Instagram, Facebook and YouTube (20,304 to 28,318 bytes) were 204 on the same build.
**Root cause.** The database's REST layer parses the body of a write as strict JSON. The compiled plan carries text of the provider's own rows (video captions, authors, comment text in `sample_rows`, `pinned_rows`, `spare_rows`); a string that holds half an emoji (a lone UTF-16 surrogate: the source cut its own text in the middle of a pair) is written by `JSON.stringify` as a lone `\udXXX` escape, which that parser refuses with «Empty or invalid json». The write was never retried with different content, so every attempt failed the same way. **#365 did not cause it** (nothing it deleted is on this path: a compiled plan goes from `planByCompiler` straight to `updateMission`; the test seam is a test-only switch that production never turns on, and the same build saved four other platforms' plans). **Not observed directly:** the page text is in Storage, which this session may not read, so the exact string is inferred (the write is refused for body content, and a lone surrogate is the one content class a JavaScript run can produce that a JSON parser refuses); the worker now logs `[agent] text_repaired ... at <keys>` (keys only, never the text) when it repairs anything, so the next occurrence names the field.
**Fix** (`src/lib/agentMission/wellFormed.ts`, used by `agent/src/supabaseStore.ts`): every write that carries outside text (`updateMission`, `addCall`, `addEvent`, the heartbeat) first replaces a lone surrogate with U+FFFD and drops a NUL (`\u0000`, which a text or JSON column cannot hold either); a clean value is passed through as the same object. The gateway's own 80-character cut of a call's inputs (`gateway.ts`, forbidden, untouched) can cut a pair the same way: its call row is covered by the same boundary. All five platforms share this write, so all five are covered.
**Tests.** `tests/round19-tiktok-wellformed.test.ts` (6) with `tests/helpers/fakeRestClient.ts` (a stand-in for the REST layer that refuses what it refused). RED on `df6e730`: the TikTok planning replay with half-emoji text on the stored M12 pages ended `Error: mission update: Empty or invalid json` at `runMission.ts:446` (the same line, the same message as live), and the store-write test failed the same way; the control (the pages as they are) passed. GREEN with the fix. The replay runs the REAL planning (compiled path, test seam OFF) into the REAL store writer.

## Round 18 (3 Nov 2026, after #364): PR B, ONE PATH FOR SOCIAL (the model-planned social pipeline is deleted)

Base `main` = `1eb58aa` (#364). **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched. Switches verified before starting (read-only SELECT on `agent_settings`, 20:19 UTC): all five social platforms «تشغيل».

**What a social request does now.** The platform's own switch decides: «تشغيل» (and «تجريبي» for a test account) = the compiled path, the only path; «إيقاف» or no value = the platform is NOT OFFERED: the customer reads `SOCIAL_NOT_OFFERED_AR` («هذه المنصة غير متاحة للطلب الآن...») BEFORE any planning call (no source read, no model paid, nothing held; `runMission.ts`; `tests/round18-one-social-path.test.ts`). A social plan proposed by the model is refused in `propose_plan`. The admin page text says so (`RedesignFlagsSection.tsx`). The type-level flags no longer decide anything for social.
**Deleted** (`agent/src/tools.ts` 292 lines, `engine/spec.ts` 62, `completeCount.ts` 306, `conditionTools.ts` 17, `engaged.ts` 13, `pageableComments.ts` 117): the country, sort/query, account-page, media, named-source and contract send-backs; the comments-recipe-by-code and comment-language passes; the complete-count estimate path (`withCompleteCount`, share, page cap, candidates; `splitPromise` stays for the plan editor); the model's topic meaning and relevance rule for social; the condition-coverage ranking and describe-first rule; `deps.codeBuild` (recipes derived from samples) and `nextToolsOf`; the `RunState` counters `countrySendBacks`, `sortSendBacks`, `accountPageSendBacks`, `mediaSendBacks`, `contractSendBacks`. Companies and places: their tests are unchanged and pass. Left in place, now unreachable from production social code (their own unit tests stay): `toolInput.ts` `buildToolInput`, `toolProfile.ts` `discoverCursor`.
**The approval guard** (`staleSocialPlan.ts`, called by `approveForCustomer` and the admin `approvePlan` BEFORE `approveMissionOnce`: no money file edited): a social plan that is not a compiled plan is refused with «انتهت صلاحية هذه الخطة؛ اطلب البحث من جديد ولن يُخصم منك شيء.» (410). One such plan was waiting at the time (read-only SELECT): `msn_1ccc7d77cc189eeb683f`, plan_ready since 30 Oct, reserve 200; none was approved, queued or executing.
**TEMPORARY test seam** (`src/lib/agentMission/testSeam.ts`, switched on for EVERY test file by `tests/setup/legacyModelPlanning.ts`, registered in `vitest.config.mts`): while on, the social gate, the social-plan refusal and the approval guard step aside, and `propose_plan` keeps the model's «no relevance rule» for flags-on social worlds (one line in `tools.ts`). It exists so the tests that drive a whole mission through a fake model transcript whose request is social (engine, settlement, restart, money) keep running. Production never turns it on (`tests/round18-one-social-path.test.ts` reads every source file). **Files that depend on it:** every test that plans through `replay()` (`tests/helpers/agentReplay.ts`) or `runPlanning()` (16 files), and the stored-plan loader there. **Remove it** when those worlds are rebuilt on compiled plans.
**Stored plans** (`tests/fixtures/stored-plans/*.json`, 29 synthetic worlds, no live data): the worlds of `s9-open-replies`, `s2-completion-text`, `s6-live-fixture`, `s11-instagram-account`, `s3-all-comments-e2e`, `raw-ingest-social` and `s1-test-account-gate` (money, settlement, keep-reading-until-promised, completion text, raw delivery, the recorded pipeline) load the STORED result of their planning (plan, pages, call log, events), captured once from the code of #364 and keyed by test name and replay number (`storedPlanning` in `agentReplay.ts`); approval, execution, settlement and the files are the code under test. Every assertion is kept except two counts in `s11` (marked CHANGED: the two listing reads and the two exposure reads of planning are no longer made in the run: listing reads in execution 2 to 0, comments reads at least 3 to at least 2).
**Tests removed with the legacy code, one by one, each tested ONLY deleted social planning behaviour (132 tests in all: 9,571 before, 9,443 after, with the 4 new ones; skipped stays 57 in the same 5 files: no skipped test was removed):** whole files `complete-count` (19), `counter-includes-replies` (5), `pageable-source-first` (6), `s2-pinned-videos` (5), `s5-promise-and-engaged` (8) (the complete-count estimate and its promise), `s2-comments-paging` (9), `s2-live-replay` (4), `s3-code-built-comments` (5) (deleted `pageableComments.ts`), `benchmark-antigravity-1-5` (5) and `round4-named-topic-choice` (7) (model-planned social plans of the five platforms; the compiled counterparts are `tests/platform-*.test.ts`, `round10-*`, `round11-*`, `round12-*`), `ranking-display` (2) (the legacy new-mode ranking display), `consolidation-matrix` (3) (the table of the deleted social consolidation rules by platform; the table in docs/S1-LIVE-FINDINGS.md is history now). Single tests: `consolidation-rules` (6: rules whose subject was the complete-count estimate and the topic meaning), `s2-real-page-cap` (3), `s2-replies-promise-final` (4), `s3-replies-requested` (1), `s4-live-fixes` (8: country send-back, replies/language passes), `s7-country-first` (11), `s8-topic-meaning` (9) (model topic meaning), `s11-instagram-account` (1: the account-page send-back; the compiled pool of two pages is `round10-instagram`), `built-request-plan` (1: recipe built by code), `contract-gate-e2e` (2: contract send-back), `cursor-discovery` (1), `plan-alternatives` (1: next tool derived from samples), `ranking-by-coverage` (1: describe-first), `mission-11-replay` 1c (1: named-source send-back), `mission-12-replay` (2: media-proof send-back), `s10-cairo-x-query` (1: day-only operators built by code), `flags-off-byte-identical` (1: the flag-ON sensitivity of a deleted mode). Changed fixture: `tests/fixtures/foundation-flags-off.json`, 20 lines, ONLY the field `planning_type_flags_test` (it now equals the flags-off digest: type flags no longer alter social planning); the `planning_flags_off` and `selector` digests (flags off byte for byte) are unchanged.
**Not proven.** (1) Nothing ran on live provider pages. (2) A social plan approved BEFORE this deploy, still waiting, would be refused by the guard; one approved and already running on the old engine is not blocked by this PR (none existed). (3) The stored plans are synthetic worlds captured from the old code: they prove the engine, settlement and files, not social planning.

## Round 17 (3 Nov 2026, after #363): live round 14 fixes (Instagram selection and mark, Facebook named page with a period, decision A suspended), PR A

Base `main` = `ae7636c` (#363). **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched. Every SELECT was read-only (list in the PR report); Storage was not read.

**1. Instagram selection (`msn_ce01cccb652036e91ca4`): NOT a regression.** The stored plan: the pool was the account's newest **24 posts of ONE hour** (20:05 to 21:12 Cairo, the card's own line); the chosen counters 4 and 3 and the two spares 3 and 2 (the plan ranks by the counter, so **the highest counter in the pool was 4**). The same code on the REAL pages of round 11's `msn_97cdab58dc82fc2039ed` (committed; two pages, 24 posts of one hour, 14:35 to 15:34 Cairo) chooses 20 and 18, and `msn_fc40280193aab21b9201` (13:07 Cairo, ONE page of 12: read before PR #356 made the pool two pages) had 45 and 9. What changed between those missions is the hour of the day and the account's posting pace, not the code: `pool.ts` has not changed since `f2c7d9d` (round 10, 14:58 Cairo, BEFORE the 20/18 mission), and the pool depth is the gateway's two samples of one listing (`GATEWAY_MAX_SAMPLES_PER_SOURCE`, `gateway.ts` untouched): a busy account's last 24 posts are about an hour, and posts that young have few comments. `tests/round17-instagram.test.ts` pins both: the stored plan's rows give exactly the stored choice and spares, and the real round-11 pages give 20 and 18. **What was added (card only, the choice is unchanged):** the line «اخترنا الأكثر تعليقًا من آخر ٢٤ منشورًا ...» now also says «أعلى عدّاد تعليقات بين ما قرأناه N، ولم نقرأ منشورات أقدم منها». A deeper pool needs the gateway's sample cap raised (forbidden file): not done, a decision for the owner.
**2. Named-source mark.** Cause: the Instagram card did not map the post's author and the compiler wrote no verdict for a listing read by the account's handle, so the raw sheet's mark was unknown and the contract's completion said «غير متاح من المصدر». Now: the Instagram card maps `user.username` (column «صاحب الحساب»); a named source read by its own listing (an Instagram account, a Facebook page by its link) gets a per-row verdict by the row's OWN author (`namedAuthorVerdict` in `pool.ts`): «مطابق» when it is the named source (a link is read by its last part), «غير مطابق» when it is someone else's, «غير متاح من المصدر» ONLY when the row carries no author (`NAMED_UNAVAILABLE_MARK_AR`; `RowMarksNote.namedUnavailable`). **R4** (every platform): «غير متاح من المصدر» in «المصدر المسمّى» beside a row that has an author is a violation, completed with «مطابق» / «غير مطابق» (`plan.compiled.source_text` is the named source, internal). Rows delivered before this PR carry no author: their cell stays what it was.
**3. Facebook named page + period, no topic (`msn_86dd9484261f41416f57`).** Cause: the mission has NO call. A keyword search needs a topic and the page listing needs the page's link; «قناة العربية» is a name, so no card was eligible and the mission was «unserved». Now: ONE read of the verified keyword search (the page's name as the keyword, the period's days) finds the page by its author's name, word for word (`pageMatchesSource`, never a filter), the listing is read by that link and walked back by its cursor (`LISTING_WALK_MAX_PAGES` 12), each row's date checked in Cairo days by code, inside the planning budget tiers and the gateway's own caps (a read the gateway refuses ends the walk; today that is the second sample of the listing, so a page that posts often is read for 6 posts). When the period's start was not reached the card says it before approval: «لم نصل إلى بداية الفترة المطلوبة ...» with the posts read and the period they cover (also for an Instagram account with a period). A name no author matches word for word gives «لم نجد صفحة باسم ...» before any plan (`page_not_found`, nothing held). A request with a page LINK needs no search read.
**4. Decision A is SUSPENDED.** `ALTERNATIVE_QUOTE_RULE_PLATFORMS` (alternatives.ts) is EMPTY: the quote is the main card's cost; the alternative is still planned and still runs after approval; the difference is ours. The 2x rule (`ALTERNATIVE_QUOTE_FACTOR`) is kept and gives its old answer when a platform is put back in that set: do it only after the alternative's real page size and cost per page are measured on real pages. Last round a 9-item Instagram request cost 218 points on the unmeasured price and the alternative was never used.
**5. The two requests to capture real pages (test account `iam.magdy+1@outlook.com`; the tester makes the paid calls; attach the admin ZIP of each mission).**
  1. `scrapecreators.instagram.post.comments` as the alternative after the primary ends short, comments only: **`هات منشورين من حساب الحدث على إنستجرام (alhadath)، مع كل التعليقات.`**, sent in the afternoon (13:00 to 16:00 Cairo), when the account's last 24 posts hold posts with 20 or more comments: in that window the primary tool (`anyapi.instagram.post.comments`) ended short on both real missions (`msn_fc40280193aab21b9201`: counters 45 and 9, delivered 35 comments; `msn_97cdab58dc82fc2039ed`: counters 20 and 18, delivered 30). At 21:12 (round 14) the counters were 4 and 3 and the primary gave everything, so the alternative was never read: do NOT send it at night. Expected cost: the card's hold about 250 to 350 points and a charge of about 180 to 230 points (those two missions charged 182 and 212; the quote is the primary card's cost again); the alternative's calls are ours: if it gives one row a page (the only page seen) a gap of 20 comments is at most 20 x 0.00188 = 0.038 USD a post, at most about 0.08 USD for the two posts. The card must say «إن توقف مصدر الجمع قبل الوصول للعدد، نحاول الإكمال من مصدر آخر للمنصة نفسها».
  2. `anyapi.facebook.post_comments` (test-account capture, comments only, no replies): **`هات منشورين من صفحة قناة العربية على فيسبوك آخر ٣ شهور، مع كل التعليقات.`** (the request of item 3, now served): the main comments tool is `scrapecreators.x.v1-facebook-post-comments` (verified); `anyapi.facebook.post_comments` is its capture alternative on a test account and is read only if the main tool ends short of a post's counter (round 13: 187 of 280), so choose the moment when the page's newest posts carry hundreds of comments (the card's line «أعلى عدّاد تعليقات» says it before approval: approve only if it is 100 or more). Expected cost: for about 300 to 500 comments the hold about 700 to 1,500 points and the charge about 300 to 800 points (round 12's comments-and-replies mission charged 2.5 points a row, comments alone are cheaper); the capture is bounded by the 0.20 USD a mission shared by catalog-only tools (decision 7.4). Round 14 captured neither tool: nothing here is measured.

**Tests.** New: `tests/round17-instagram.test.ts`, `tests/round17-facebook-page-period.test.ts`. RED RUN on the code of #363 (recorded): 15 of the 25 tests of the two new files and the changed Decision A test failed; the selection tests passed on the unchanged code (that IS the proof of item 1). CHANGED existing test: `tests/round16-alternatives-plan.test.ts` (Decision A: the quote is the main card's cost; the 2x rule is checked through its parameter).

## Round 16 (3 Nov 2026, after #362): the compiled comments stage continues on another tool of the same platform; «الشهر الماضي»; the cards' inputs checked against the catalog

Base `main` = `7aa0f66` (#362). One branch, one PR. **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched (`price.ts` is READ through `stagePriceInputs` / `priceMissionByCode`).

**ROOT CAUSE (confirmed in the code).** The engine already continues across tools: `agent/src/engine/run.ts` 205-219 (a child stage's `spec.alternatives`, after a read that failed, ran dry or only repeated itself) and 396-420 (a root stage), and a compiled plan always runs under `redesignStages` (`redesign.ts` 15). But `spec.alternatives` was only ever set by the legacy builder (`engine/spec.ts` 213-219, 246-293); the compiler built its stages without it (`compilePlan.ts` parents 353-361, comments 422-433, replies 448-456 and 472-478). So a compiled comments stage ended where its one tool ended: Instagram `msn_97cdab58dc82fc2039ed` «سُلّم ٣٤ من ٥٤ لأن المصدر لم يعد يعطي», Facebook round 13 «سُلّم ١٨٧ من ٢٨٠». Proof: `tests/round16-alternatives-engine.test.ts` (synthetic) passes on the UNCHANGED engine as soon as a plan carries `alternatives`; nothing in the engine was changed.

**1. Compiled comments alternatives** (`foundation/alternatives.ts`, pure, decided before approval, no planning call). A card is kept only if: same platform and exact role, another endpoint, `verified` with stored evidence (or `catalog_only` on a TEST account), its parent input binds to a value every pinned parent already carries (explicit `from`), no `parent_binding.also` or grandparent field (the engine forwards neither), the comments mapping, the same kind of date as the main card, every identity or token a planned replies card needs (Facebook `feedback_id` + `expansion_token`, YouTube's reply token), and its comment ids PROVEN equal to the main card's on stored pages (`COMMENT_ID_PROOFS`, recomputed by `tests/round16-comment-id-proofs.test.ts`). At most two; order: proven before capture, fewest conditions it cannot carry, a paged source before a one-page one, cheaper row, endpoint id. Every exclusion has a code (`status`, `no_evidence`, `no_parent_input`, `needs_also`, `parent_value_missing`, `mapping`, `replies_identity`, `date_semantics`, `ids_unproven`). The engine reads each alternative as its own stream (its own cursor, from the parent's remaining allowance, per-parent and total caps and the pinned parents unchanged), only after a failed read, an exhausted source or a source that only repeats itself, never past a budget or daily-cap refusal; the item store keeps the first record of an id (exact identity only).
  **Which platforms get one today (confirmed from the code, with one CORRECTION to the brief):** Instagram: yes (`anyapi.instagram.post.comments` main, `scrapecreators.instagram.post.comments` alternative; ids equal on the one real pair of pages M15 calls 3 and 4: ONE comment, thin evidence). Facebook, TikTok: no (one verified comments card each). X: no comments card. **YouTube: NONE (correction):** the non-paginated card is left out with replies asked (no reply token) AND without them (`date_semantics`: the main card's dates are English relative text turned into a marked approximate day, the other card gives no date, so a file would mix dates its limits line does not explain). A test account on Facebook, comments only, gets `anyapi.facebook.post_comments` as a CAPTURE (decision 2): its ids are NOT proven equal to the main card's (no stored page exists), so a repeated comment under another id would be delivered twice on that test file; that is what the live round measures. A capture shares the 0.20 USD cap of decision 7.4: the stage's `max_pages` is the smaller of the caps of its cards (stage-wide, so on a test account it also bounds the main card).
**Decision A (pricing).** The approved price never changes and the difference is ours, EXCEPT when the dearest compatible alternative costs more than 2x the main tool per expected row: then the stage's `measured_cost_per_row_usd` is that alternative's, through the one pricing path. **Today this changes Instagram quotes only** (scrapecreators 0.00188 USD a row against 0.000288: 6.5x, and its `observedRowsPerPage` is 1 from one single-comment page, so the ratio is pessimistic): the M16 request (2 posts, comments and replies, 18 rows) quotes 16.75 points a row and a hold of 347 (206 before), the M15 request 22.32 and 257 (20 and 200 before). Facebook (test), TikTok, X, YouTube: unchanged.

**3. Card and file honesty.** The card says «إن توقف مصدر الجمع قبل الوصول للعدد، نحاول الإكمال من مصدر آخر للمنصة نفسها، داخل حدود الخطة المعتمدة.» only when the stage has an alternative; and, when the customer named a source, a country or a language, «من العناصر المختارة وعددها N: M تطابق شروطك، و K منها بدائل عليها علامة في الملف» counted on the planning rows (a separate sentence; the older unmet-conditions sentence is untouched). When every route was tried and a parent is still short, its gap line says the expected count, the unique delivered, the gap, and that the main route and N alternative routes were tried (`alternativesGapLineAr`, white label), from the item store's persisted streams only (`MissionFacts.stages[k].routes`, `alternativeRoutesOf`); a route that left no stream (never called) is not claimed. **R2** of the delivery contract requires that statement after a real shortfall with alternatives tried (`ContractInput.routes`); with no alternative on record R2 is what it was.

**4. «الشهر الماضي» / «الشهر اللي فات»** (`ask.ts`): the previous calendar month in Cairo time (3 Nov 2026 gives 1 to 31 Oct 2026; year boundary, leap February tested); `requestConditions.ts` now also reads «اللي فات» and «الماضى»; «آخر شهر» stays the last 30 days.
**5. Static contract** (`tests/round16-card-inputs-declared.test.ts`): every input any Tool Card sends (query, period, source, ids, country, order, page size, cursor, parent and `also`, `fixed`) is declared in the catalog snapshot or `DECLARED_EXTRA_INPUTS` (the gateway's own `known` set). It was green at once (no card strips an input today); its negative case shows it catches one. `gateway.ts` is not edited.

**Tests.** New: `tests/round16-{alternatives-unit,alternatives-plan,alternatives-engine,gap-line-r2,match-counts,previous-month,card-inputs-declared,comment-id-proofs,replays-unchanged}.test.ts`, `tests/helpers/round14Missions.ts` (the four stored missions, shared with `round14-contract-replay`). RED RUN first (recorded): the unit file failed to load (no module), the month file failed 8 of 11; the engine file was green on the unchanged engine (that IS the root-cause proof). Changed existing lines (marked CHANGED): `tests/platform-instagram.test.ts` (the M16 engine replay now also calls the planned alternative, which no stored page answers: the only unserved calls are that card's, the 13 comments are still the stored ones) and `tests/round11-tiktok-x-invariance.test.ts` (one more stated diff set apart before the pinned hash: the new matching-counts sentence; the pinned digests are unchanged). The offline replays of TikTok M12, YouTube round 11 and 12, Facebook round 12 are unchanged (no alternative, same rows, zero violations).

**Not proven.** (1) No stored page shows an Instagram `scrapecreators` comments page of a parent that `anyapi` ended short for, so the continuation is proven by synthetic control-flow tests, not on a real pair. (2) `scrapecreators.instagram.post.comments` real page size and cursor on a page WITH a next page: unseen (`observedRowsPerPage` 1, so the quote is pessimistic). (3) `anyapi.facebook.post_comments` has never answered with rows.
**Next live round (test account).** One Instagram request likely to exhaust the primary comments source (a post with more comments than `anyapi` gives, all the comments of 2 posts); one Facebook comments-only request (no replies) on the test account, all the comments of a post whose counter is above 187; one request with «الشهر الماضي». Attach the admin ZIP: the same-parent pages of both Instagram cards and the first `anyapi.facebook.post_comments` pages are what promote the evidence.

## Round 15 (3 Nov 2026, after #361): the delivery contract, the count and budget question, and the real pages of live round 12

Base `main` = `bc7cdc1` (#361). One branch, one PR. **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts` (read only), `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched. The compiled-path changes run only for a platform whose own flag is on (or a test account); the old path's files are byte for byte what they were (`tests/flags-off-byte-identical.test.ts` passes unchanged).

**The ZIP** (`diger-raw-pages-20261103-1646.zip`, read in the scratch folder only): 102 files = `manifest.json` + Facebook `msn_236f4af6fe4cb890abee` 82 (79 raw pages, 3 rows files) + YouTube `msn_5d5a9b2a933aeaa47d81` 19 (16 raw, 3 rows). All 101 are committed, anonymised with `tests/fixtures/live/anonymise.py` (random salt, never written); the privacy scan passes; its inventory test is CHANGED to 36 missions, 667 files (594 raw, 73 rows). Their 94 calls are in `call-index.json` (read-only SELECT on `mission_calls`; paging and reply tokens the log cuts at 80 characters are rebuilt from the stored pages in call order and match the logged ones; a `pfbid` link is indexed by a numeric surrogate of its first 24 characters).

**PART C.** (1) **`anyapi.facebook.comment_replies` is VERIFIED** on the 36 real pages (calls 46 to 81): list path `output.data.replies`, ids unique, one call per comment that has replies (the order of the comments pages), the parent binding is the comment's `feedback_id` and `expansion_token` (the first 80 characters the log keeps match), `hasNextPage` false on each (one page ends a thread, `nextCursor` is a text), 95 rows in 36 calls = 2.64 a call (1 to 7); four comments gave fewer than their counters (4 for 3, 6 for 5, 7 for 6, 8 for 6: 100 counted, 95 returned). `observedRowsPerPage` is **2.6** (the measured rate rounded down). Effect: a Facebook replies step is now in EVERY account's plan (it was a test-account tool), and the replies are priced at 0.0012 / 2.6 USD a row instead of 0.0012: the round-11 plan's hold falls from 26,653 to 12,734 (it fits the cap). The other replies tool was never used and stays `catalog_only`. (2) **YouTube published_time arrived in English:** on the 123 stored comment rows «11 months ago» 120 times, «9 months ago» twice, «11 months ago (edited)» once (round 13's synthetic form is now real); the replies' `publishedTime` «11 months ago» on 4; the videos «N years ago» and «Streamed 6 years ago». The round-12 file rebuilt from these pages with this code has «دقة التاريخ» = «تقريبي» on 99 of 99 comment and reply rows and 2 of 2 videos (`tests/round14-youtube-english-real.test.ts`).

**PART A, the delivery contract** (`src/lib/agentMission/foundation/deliveryContract.ts`, pure). R1 one number (card, file, chat, notice, orders page say the same Y; X equals the data rows), R2 every gap stated (a line per parent with both numbers and its ordinal, a stage line, «لا قيود سُجّلت» only when no gap), R3 approximate dates marked (the row's «دقة التاريخ» and a count line), R4 marks filled (every condition the request set has its column on the parent sheet and no blank or «غير محدد» cell). It is not a filter: it never removes a row, never changes a value, never blocks (it runs in a try/catch). In delivery (`buildMissionFiles`, not `finalize.ts`) R2, R3 and R4 only COMPLETE the description for a compiled plan (a limits line, a precision cell, a blank mark cell filled from the row or «غير متاح من المصدر»); R1 only reports; the worker records an internal event (`adm_contract`, rules, what was completed); `/admin/agent` has a read-only section «ملفات صُحّحت تلقائيًا» (the last 20, fed by `loadOverview`). `promisedTotalOf` (new) is the ONE formula of «تم تسليم X من Y» used by the file and the notice.
**The replay** (`tests/round14-contract-replay.test.ts`, `tests/helpers/missionReplay.ts`: the compiler plans from the stored planning pages, the engine reads the stored execution pages, the file is built as the worker builds it) runs on the 4 stored missions that have the pages of both phases: TikTok M12, YouTube round 11 and round 12, Facebook round 12 (the live 95 replies, 366 comments, 3 posts are reproduced exactly). Not replayed, with reasons: X `msn_b060d3347ed7660f972e` (one execution page), X `msn_50e3b83c44e7ebd7f577` (replies pages only), Instagram `msn_97cdab58dc82fc2039ed` (the delivered comments pages are not stored), Facebook `msn_e3e7632e2fb2d42f9c19` (planning pages incomplete), Facebook `msn_9d196025e7929324983a` (never approved). Because the anonymised titles carry no topic word, the live choice of videos and posts cannot be ranked: the live two videos are pinned (YouTube), the live three posts get their authors and the topic words back in the planning pages (Facebook); stated in the test.
**RED RUN on the code of #361** (every violation, before any fix): TikTok M12: R1 card 302 against 406 on the file, the chat, the notice and the orders page; YouTube round 11 and round 12: R1 card 97 against 101 on the same four; Facebook round 12: R1 card 559 against **1,327** on the same four (the first fix of #361, «the replies promise is `expected_rows`», read the ESTIMATE the hold is made from (586 on the live plan) because the compiler writes `expected_rows` = `planned_rows` for open replies: #361's test used a fixture with 17 and never had the real shape) and R2: five missing per-parent lines (posts 1, 2 and 3 against their counters 16, 379, 122 for 5, 311, 50 delivered, and the replies of posts 2 and 3: counters 47 and 52, delivered 45 and 49).
**What was fixed (code, not the test):** the replies promise of every compiled plan is the number the card showed (`compiled.promises.<key>.counted`, 0 when none: a stage that promised no number is not in the card's total, and after the run it is judged to what the source gave only for comments with no counter); `promisedRowsOf` follows the card's own rule (open stages out, counted replies in); a delivery above its promise says why («٤٠٦ ... أكثر من ... المحسوبة»); the Facebook parent lines name the parent («المنشور رقم ١: ...») and carry the replies counters against the replies delivered (compiled plans only); TikTok, X and Instagram follow the same rule (D3: «٦٣ من ٦٦» is now «٦٣ من ٧٠» on the replay of those pages: the card's own number). Result: zero violations on the four missions.

**PART B, the count and the budget** (`foundation/countChoice.ts`, `budgetFit.ts`; `compiled.ts`, `runMission.ts`, `runState.ts`). A request with no number of videos, posts or tweets is asked «تحب كام؟» BEFORE any read (nothing is called or priced): ١٠، ٥٠، ١٠٠، a typed number, or «حدد ميزانية بالنقاط» (then «كم نقطة ميزانيتك؟»); comments asked with no number per parent and not «كل التعليقات» are asked «كم تعليقًا تريد من كل ...؟» («كل التعليقات», ١٠, ٥٠, ١٠٠ or a number). The choices are kept in the run note (each answer replaces `answer_text`); at most 3 cards in all (a fourth new question is not asked: today's defaults apply). A **budget** (said in the request: «بميزانية ٣٠٠٠ نقطة», «ميزانيتي», «بحد أقصى», «لا تتجاوز», «في حدود» with «نقطة»; or chosen) is a hard ceiling on the HOLD (and never above the job cap): `fitToBudget` compiles the Ask, then, when it does not fit, one comment per parent for every number of parents from the asked one down to one and the most comments per parent that still fit (binary search), each candidate priced by the compiler's own call of `priceMissionByCode` (read only); the card says «ميزانيتك ... تكفي تقريبًا لـ N ... مع حتى M تعليق من كل ...، والحجز المؤقت المطلوب H ولا يتجاوز ميزانيتك»; below one parent and one comment there is no plan and the sentence says so. **Charges unchanged.** The budget from «the card»: the choice «حدد ميزانية بالنقاط» on the question card (the plan editor has no budget field: not built). A request that already has a count and no budget gives the same plan, byte for byte (tested); a planning that does not switch the question on never asks.
**What a TikTok, X or Instagram customer sees differently** (their flags are on): (1) a request with no number of items now gets the «تحب كام؟» card first (the count was 10 by default); (2) comments asked with no number per parent and no «كل» get one question (the default was every comment); (3) a budget in the words is obeyed; (4) the promised number after a run is the plan card's («٦٣ من ٧٠» not «٦٣ من ٦٦» on the TikTok D3 pages) and a delivery above it says why; (5) the file: «دقة التاريخ» beside any approximate date, «المصدر المسمّى» reads «مطابق» / «غير مطابق», a blank mark the request set (TikTok M12: 2 country cells of the videos sheet) reads «غير متاح من المصدر», the first sheet's limits lines name the parent when they are the compiled plan's; X's data sheets are unchanged (hash pinned), the plan carries one internal field more (`compiled.conditions`).

**Tests.** New: `tests/round14-{contract-replay,contract-unit,count-budget,facebook-replies-real,youtube-english-real}.test.ts`, helper `tests/helpers/missionReplay.ts`; `replayGateway` gained `cut80`, `phase` and the pfbid surrogate (all off by default). Run red first: 25 of the new tests failed on the old code; the contract's red run is listed above. Changed existing test lines (marked CHANGED): `tests/fixtures-anonymised.test.ts`, `tests/platform-cards.test.ts`, `tests/platform-facebook.test.ts`, `tests/round11-facebook-limit.test.ts` (the card's measured rate put back to 1 for this file only), `tests/round13-facebook-replies-measure.test.ts`, `tests/round11-tiktok-x-invariance.test.ts`, `tests/live-d-delivery.test.ts`, `tests/round10-instagram.test.ts`, `tests/round14-promised-number.test.ts`.

**Not proven.** (1) The contract's completions were exercised on made-up deliveries, never on a live one (the four live replays have zero violations, so nothing was completed). (2) The replay's Facebook plan counts 39 replies at planning (25 comments read) where the live plan counted 17 (15): the replay reads the stored exposure pages with the current exposure rule. (3) Facebook reply thread of more than one page, YouTube reply thread of more than one page: never seen. (4) The count question was tested on the stored TikTok pages and the run note, not through a live `needs_input` round trip in a browser. (5) The budget has no field in the plan editor.
