## Round 14 (3 Nov 2026, after #360): live round 12 of the test account (Facebook `msn_236f4af6fe4cb890abee`, YouTube `msn_5d5a9b2a933aeaa47d81`), nine findings

Base `main` = `9822bcd` (#360). One branch, one PR. **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts` (read only), `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched. Compiled-path changes are behind the platform flags; UI changes (5 to 8) apply to every customer.

**Every SELECT of the diagnosis** (none wrote): S1 `missions` created after 14:30 UTC (the two ids, status, answer, delivered, charged, reserved); S2 `information_schema.columns` of `mission_calls`, `mission_events`; S3 `mission_calls` of the YouTube mission (endpoint, phase, status, rows, cost, `params_digest`); S4 `credit_ledger` joined to `missions` on `job_id` for both missions; S5 `mission_events` of both (no progress/call rows); S6 `plans_quotes` and `jobs` of the test account after 15:35 UTC; S7 `missions.plan_public` and `plan` of the Facebook mission (stage `expected_rows`, `planned_rows`, `compiled.promises`, price); S8 `mission_calls` of the Facebook mission grouped by endpoint, phase, status; S9 a count of missions in `executing / planning / approved / queued`. Vercel runtime logs and runtime errors (15:30 to 16:10 UTC) were read; Storage was not.

**1. YouTube dates: which card, which path, why the PR #360 mark was not seen.** The comments tool `tikhub.youtube.video.comments` WAS asked with `language_code: "en"` on all 12 of its calls (S3: `params_digest` carries it). The dates «2025-12-03 00:00:00» are the approximate DAY computed from «11 months ago»: path `mapping (relativeDates: raw text kept) -> ingestRows -> parseRelativeAge -> approxDayOf`, a date-only cell. So the English words DID come and the parse WORKED (this is the first real proof of the English forms). **Why no «تقريبًا»:** the word lived ONLY in the cell's number format; whoever reads the values (pandas, openpyxl, a filter, a copy, the reviewer) sees «2025-12-03 00:00:00». The replies (`anyapi.youtube.comment_replies`, 4 rows) were worse: their `createdUtc` is an INSTANT the provider computed (the minute of the call minus 11 CALENDAR months, e.g. `1764769197` = 2025-12-03 13:39:57 UTC beside `publishedTime` «11 months ago», real page of round 11), shown as «2025-12-03 17:52:00» (the live call was ~15:52 UTC); the existing computed-date check used 30.4375-day months (it never matched a calendar month) and the replies mapping did not keep `publishedTime` at all. **The live file itself was not read (Storage): this is reconstructed from S3, the code and the real pages of the same videos; the owner's ZIP of `msn_5d5a9b2a933aeaa47d81` confirms it.** **Fix (every platform):** a new column «دقة التاريخ» right after any date column that holds any approximate date: «تقريبي» for a computed day, «دقيق» for a real moment, empty when there is no date; the date cell stays a real date (sortable); the number format keeps «تقريبًا». Chosen over a text-only date («~2025-12-03», breaks sorting) and over a note on the first sheet (not per row). «حدود هذا التسليم» now says «N من M تاريخًا تقريبيًا ... مُعلَّم بكلمة «تقريبي» في عمود «دقة التاريخ»». `derivedRelativeDay` recognises calendar months and years; the YouTube replies card keeps `publishedTime` as `date_text` (card flag `relativeDates`, stage flag `relative_dates`). A file with no approximate date has no new column. **Why the same 101 rows and 97 points as round 11:** they are NEW paid calls (16 calls, the same 2 videos `gCut2DcB9sM` and `GIWkArP7RJE`, `language_code` added; S3), not reused data: the comments tool answers "top comments" of a video in a stable order, so the same 95 comments, 4 replies and 2 videos came back; the charge is `ceil(101 x unit price)` of the same plan shape (hold 242, charge 97).

**2. Facebook named-source mark.** Cause: the compiler wrote its verdict into a plan column (`named_source`: «» for the named page, «المصدر المسمّى: غير مطابق» for a fill), and the raw sheet's own mark column «المصدر المسمّى» is computed from an `account` filter that a compiled Facebook plan never has: «غير محدد» on all 3. Now `markVerdict` reads the row's own verdict when there is no `account` filter; the column is on every named-page Facebook plan (also when all three are the page's); the plan's own column is not repeated beside the mark column; the cells read «مطابق» / «غير مطابق» / «غير محدد» on EVERY platform (TikTok, X and Instagram, which have the `account` filter, used «نعم» / «لا»: the diff is those cells only, `tests/social-mark-columns.test.ts` CHANGED).

**3. Facebook count, row by row (S7).** BEFORE (the card): 3 posts + 517 comments (the posts' counters) + 17 replies (`counted`: the reply counters of the 15 comments read at planning) = **537**; the other 569 replies (586 - 17) were only the estimate the hold is made from. AFTER (round 11's rule, `withRepliesJudged`): the replies stage was re-judged against the reply counters of the 366 delivered comments (they add up to 100) and written back into the public plan: 3 + 517 + 100 = **620**; delivered 3 + 366 + 95 = **464**. The test reproduced «٤٦٤ من ٦٢٠» exactly on the old code. Now the replies promise of a compiled FACEBOOK plan is the card's number, so «٤٦٤ من ٥٣٧» on the card, the chat, the file, the notice and the orders page; the 95 delivered against the counters' 100 stay in the limits lines. TikTok, X and Instagram keep the judged promise (`FROZEN_PROMISE_PLATFORMS`; the TikTok D3 live test is unchanged). 366 of 517 comments were delivered: the posts' counters count what the platform shows, the tool gave fewer (the limits lines say so).

**4. The approve button.** Ledger (S4): exactly ONE `res_`, ONE `rel_` and ONE `chg_` per mission; Facebook reserve 5749, release 5749, charge 2098 (464 rows); YouTube reserve 242, release 242, charge 97 (101 rows); S6: one quote and one job per mission. So the first click NEVER reached the quote or the reserve, nothing was charged twice, and the server logged no error (runtime errors: none) and answers JSON on every path. **The browser error text itself was not seen, so the exact cause is NOT proven.** What the code shows before the money step: `authHeaders()` swallows a failed `getSession()` and then POSTs with NO token (a 401 on the first click while the session refreshes), and the catch of `act()` said «لم يُخصم شيء» even when the answer was only lost. Runtime logs also show a CSP report `script-src blocked=eval` at each page load (15:39:24 and 15:44:04), unrelated to the click. **Fix (client only, no money file):** `src/lib/missionApprove.ts`: a first request that never completes, or answers 401/502/503/504, is retried ONCE after a session refresh (the server claim is idempotent: one row changes `plan_ready -> approved`, deterministic ids); «already approved» (409) and a lost answer are settled by reading the mission; «لم يُخصم شيء» is said only when the mission still waits. **If it happens again, the tester writes the exact text and the status of the request in the browser's network panel.**

**5. Plan card at 390 px.** Cause: the value of a step was `shrink-0` beside the label, so a long value kept its whole width and squeezed the label to nothing. Measured in Chromium at 390 px on the card markup (scratch script, the same markup the tests render): BEFORE the page was 523 px wide, the label 0 px wide (133 px tall: letter by letter), the value started at -133 px; AFTER the page is 390 px, the label 297 px, the value 297 px inside the card on two lines. Label and value stack below 640 px; sample table headers wrap with a minimum width inside their own scrolling box.

**6. Wallet estimate.** The old chips used the per-record table of the old calculator. Now `src/lib/walletEstimate.ts` uses `priceMissionByCode` (read only) with the verified cards' cost per row, finds the largest request whose HOLD fits, respects the minimum order (200 points: `priceMissionByCode` gives a 200-point hold for any small order) and says «رصيدك أقل من الحد الأدنى للطلب (٢٠٠ نقطة)» below it; 113 points gives no chip. YouTube comments are priced with the 2 videos and 25% replies (so it never promises more than the live plan: 242 points held 200 comments and 20 replies, the estimate for 242 is at most 200). Companies are not promised (no verified card). The old table stays on the public pricing page (not changed).

**7. Queue notice.** The live YouTube request waited 4.5 minutes (created 15:44:11, planning started 15:48:45) behind the customer's OWN Facebook run (S5). Now `readQueue` counts the customer's own running and waiting requests (read only), and the line says why: «طلبك ينتظر لأن لديك طلبًا جاريًا؛ سيبدأ تلقائيًا بعد انتهائه.», or the count of other customers' requests ahead, never a time and never «ترتيب». The second line is «ولن يُخصم شيء قبل موافقتك.» The e-mail rule (one e-mail only when the measured wait exceeds a minute) is in `src/functions/**` and was not touched.

**8. Orders page.** Running requests first; the plans that wait are ONE line («٤ خطط تنتظر اعتمادك») that expands (`OpenMissionsList`); one status word per state («في الانتظار», «قيد الدراسة», «معتمدة», «قيد التنفيذ») with a note that never repeats it and states no time; the tab «حجز مؤقت مُحرَّر» keeps its approved word and says what it is under the tabs when selected.

**9. Facebook replies measurement (S8, the first run with real reply rows).**

| tool | phase | calls | rows | rows per call | most in a call | USD |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `anyapi.facebook.comment_replies` | execution | 36 | 95 | 2.64 | 7 | 0.0432 |
| `scrapecreators.x.v1-facebook-post-comment-replies` | none | 0 | 0 | not used | | |
| (comments tool `scrapecreators.x.v1-facebook-post-comments`, for reference) | execution | 38 | 366 | 9.63 | 10 | 0.07144 |

The card sizes a reply row as ONE call (`observedRowsPerPage` 1, 0.0012 USD a row); measured, a call gave 2.64 rows, so a reply row costs 0.0012 / 2.64 = 0.000455 USD. **The round-11 plan's hold at the measured rate:** 4,879 rows (3 posts, 1,896 comments, 2,980 replies); provider cost 4.188 USD (replies 3.576) falls to 0.612 + 2,980 x 0.000455 = 1.9665 USD; with the model share (0.026 USD) the unit price falls from 4.750125 to about 2.246 points, the maximum charge from 23,176 to about 10,960 and **the hold from 26,653 to about 12,600** (about 53% lower; this plan was over the 25,000 cap, this one fits). **Recommendation (nothing switched):** keep `anyapi.facebook.comment_replies` as the replies tool (36 real calls, no failure; the other was never needed); after the owner attaches the ZIP of `msn_236f4af6fe4cb890abee` and the reply pages are committed as anonymised fixtures, verify the card on them and set its `observedRowsPerPage` to the measured rate (about 2.6, conservatively 2.5), which lowers the hold; until then it stays `catalog_only` (test accounts). One mission is one sample: threads vary (most 7 in a call).

**Tests.** New: `tests/round14-{dates,named-mark,promised-number,approve-recovery,mobile-card,wallet-estimate,queue-notice,orders-page}.test.*` (30 of them failed on the old code, run red first). Changed existing test lines (marked CHANGED): `tests/round13-youtube-dates.test.ts` (the replies' dates), `tests/social-mark-columns.test.ts`, `tests/ui-pass-1.test.tsx`, `tests/agent-customer-view.test.ts`, `tests/audit-phase1-flows.test.tsx`, `tests/requests-page-replay.test.ts`.

**Not proven.** (1) The real cause of the first-click error (the recovery is generic; the browser text is needed). (2) The live files themselves (Storage): the date and mark findings are reconstructed; the ZIPs of both missions confirm them and give fixtures. (3) Facebook reply pages: none committed. (4) The CSP `eval` report. (5) A real phone: the card was measured in headless Chromium at 390 px only.

## Round 13 (3 Nov 2026, after #359): YouTube comment dates, the engine replayed on the real YouTube pages, the Facebook replies measurement, and the two requests of the next live round

Base `main` = `e7f3491` (#359). One branch, one PR. **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call (none was made here).** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched. TikTok, X and Instagram are not changed: the map code of their cards is byte for byte what it was (the new parameter of `buildReplyMapCode` is off for them and the invariance tests pass); Facebook is not changed either. Everything below is YouTube and compiled-plan only, behind the YouTube flag (`youtube` of `redesign_flags`, or `test` for a test account); flags off, the model plans as before.

**1. YouTube comment dates.** The verified comments card `tikhub.youtube.video.comments` answers `published_time` as a relative text in the language of `language_code` (default `zh-CN`: «11个月前»; round 12 left the date empty). Now: (a) the card sends `language_code: "en"` on every call (`inputs.fixed`); it IS a declared input of the catalog entry (`inputs`: `language_code`, query, «default zh-CN»), so nothing was added to `DECLARED_EXTRA_INPUTS` and the pinned snapshot is untouched. (b) The mapping (card flag `mapping.relativeDates`, `buildReplyMapCode(..., relativeText)`) keeps the source's text instead of dropping it; the compiled comments stage carries `relative_dates: true`. (c) `ingestRows` reads the text against the moment of the read with the existing `parseRelativeAge` («3 days ago», «11 months ago», «1 year ago», «2 weeks ago», «5 hours ago», and now a trailing «(edited)»): the cell is the approximate Cairo day and the row carries the existing `_approx_dates` mark, so the file shows «تقريبًا yyyy-mm-dd» and the first sheet carries the approximate-dates note. A text it cannot read (the Chinese one, «two moons ago», more than 1000 units) leaves the cell EMPTY (never the foreign words) and the row carries a new internal mark `_date_unread`; «حدود هذا التسليم» says «N من M سُلّمت بلا تاريخ ...» (Arabic-Indic numerals). The replies come from another tool (`anyapi.youtube.comment_replies`, real instants) and are not touched. **The English forms are SYNTHETIC: no real page of the comments tool asked with `language_code: "en"` is stored (no such call was ever made); the tests write the English forms over the real pages. Whether the provider really answers «3 days ago» for `language_code: "en"` is NOT proven until the live round.** The date is computed from the moment the page was mapped (seconds after the call), as for the other approximate dates. Red first: 4 tests failed on the old behaviour (the card had no fixed input, the stage sent none, the Chinese cells were not counted, the English forms were not read); green after. Tests: `tests/round13-youtube-dates.test.ts`; one round 12 test line is CHANGED (`tests/round12-youtube-verify.test.ts`: the mapping now carries the text; the delivered cell is what is pinned).

**2. The engine replayed end to end on the real pages of `msn_41a73a6b2c0b4a76ef0a`** (`runEngine` on the compiled plan, the stored pages only, no network). **Result: delivered rows equal the stored `rows/*.json`: 2 videos, 95 comments, 4 replies; the same ids, each comment under the same video and each reply under the same comment; no page was asked that the live mission had not read.** Three differences found, none of them in the engine: (i) **the video choice cannot be reproduced from the committed pages**: the live plan pinned `gCut2DcB9sM` and `GIWkArP7RJE` (SELECT on `missions.plan`) on the REAL titles; the committed titles are anonymised (`نص_...`, no topic word), so the compiler on them keeps the source's order and chooses `VZqp9F8ed2s` and `lLAE4Iy-JNI` (the same on `6197074`, so it is not a round 12 change). The test pins the live two as the plan's pinned rows and says so; (ii) the live call log has the planning and the execution read of the same first comments page (calls 6 and 8) with DIFFERENT contents (the execution page has one comment the planning page did not): the replay gateway gained `phase` (serve only the calls of one phase; absent = as before), without it the replay misses that comment; (iii) the call log keeps 80 characters of a token, so the replay gateway gained `cut80` (compare those inputs on their first 80 characters; absent = as before). The comments tool is compared by `video_id` and `continuation_token` only because the stored calls did not send `language_code`.

**3. Facebook replies measurement (test accounts only).** No tool was switched and no call was made. Both Facebook replies tools are `catalog_only`, so only a test account's compiled plan reads Facebook replies. Every call the engine makes is one `mission_calls` row with `rows_returned`, `phase`, `status`, `cost_usd` (pinned for both tools by `tests/round13-facebook-replies-measure.test.ts`, with fake answers). After the live round, the report states rows per call for each tool with this read-only SELECT (the mission id of the Facebook request):

```sql
select endpoint_id, phase, status, count(*) as calls, sum(rows_returned) as rows, round(avg(rows_returned)::numeric, 2) as rows_per_call, max(rows_returned) as max_rows, sum(cost_usd) as usd
from mission_calls
where mission_id = '<facebook mission id>' and endpoint_id in ('anyapi.facebook.comment_replies', 'scrapecreators.x.v1-facebook-post-comment-replies')
group by 1, 2, 3 order by 1, 2, 3;
```

**4. The two live requests of the round (test account; the owner's tester makes the paid calls):**

1. `هات منشورات عن الذكاء الاصطناعي من صفحة قناة العربية على فيسبوك آخر ٣ شهور، ٣ منشورات، مع كل التعليقات والردود.`
2. `هات فيديوهين على يوتيوب عن نهائي السوبر المصري الأهلي والزمالك من القنوات الرسمية، مع كل التعليقات والردود.`

**Not proven:** (1) that the provider answers English relative texts for `language_code: "en"` (synthetic until the live round); (2) a YouTube reply thread of more than one page; (3) Facebook reply rows per call (no reply row was ever seen); (4) the video choice of the replay (pinned to the live one, see item 2); (5) the live pages were not read (Storage): no raw page was read this round.

What to check, request 2 (YouTube), in the file and the call log:
- The comments calls in `mission_calls.params` carry `language_code: "en"`.
- The admin ZIP of the mission is attached to the next session: the real `published_time` strings (do they read «3 days ago», «11 months ago (edited)»? in English at all?) become fixtures, and the synthetic English forms of this round are replaced by the real ones.
- «تاريخ ووقت التعليق»: a readable text is «تقريبًا yyyy-mm-dd» (and the first sheet carries the approximate-dates note); an unreadable one is an EMPTY cell; no cell holds Chinese or English words. «حدود هذا التسليم» says how many comments have no date («N من M سُلّمت بلا تاريخ»); that number equals the empty date cells of the comments sheet.
- The two videos are the official clubs' (the card says it cannot verify that), the per-video limit of 100 comments, the replies of each comment against its counter (the lines of «عدّاد المصدر وما سُلّم»); a reply thread of more than one page, if one occurs (never seen so far).
- The occurrence question («آخر مرة (الافتراضي)») is asked first; answer it with the default.

What to check, request 1 (Facebook):

## Round 12 (3 Nov 2026, after #358): the real pages of the three round-11 missions (YouTube cards, Instagram zero, Facebook period)

Base `main` = `78e79ac` (#358). One branch, one PR. **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched. The owner's admin ZIP (`diger-raw-pages-20261103-1444.zip`, 65 files: YouTube 19 = 16 raw + 3 rows, Instagram 40 = 37 raw + 3 rows, Facebook 5 raw; manifest equal) was read in the scratch folder only; 33 of its files are committed, anonymised with `tests/fixtures/live/anonymise.py` (YouTube all 19, Instagram raw calls 1 to 6 and the 3 rows files, Facebook the 5 raw pages; its KEEP list gained three keys: `reply_continuation_token` (a paging token like `continuation_token`), `like_count` and `reply_count` (counters written as text); the privacy scan passes, its inventory test is CHANGED to 34 missions and 566 files). The calls were added to `call-index.json` (read-only SELECTs on `mission_calls` of the three missions; the replies calls' tokens are cut at 80 characters by the call log).

**1. YouTube cards: both VERIFIED.** On the real pages: `tikhub.youtube.video.comments` answers `data.comments` (20 a page; 8 pages: 123 comments of two videos), each with `comment_id`, `content`, `reply_count` and `like_count` AS TEXT, `reply_continuation_token` on the 3 comments that have replies, and `data.continuation_token` (calls 8 to 12 are one chain: each call asked for exactly the token the page before gave; null on the last page of each video); the parent link is `video_id` (echoed by the page); the card's own check finds no issue on any page. `anyapi.youtube.comment_replies` answers `output.data.comments` (1, 1 and 2 rows for the comments whose counters said 1, 1 and 2), the reply `id` is `<comment id>.<reply id>`, `createdUtc` is a real instant, `nextCursor` is an empty text on the last page, and the call's input is the comment's token (its first 80 characters match). **What differed, and is fixed:** the comments tool's `published_time` is a RELATIVE TEXT IN CHINESE («11个月前»), because the tool's `language_code` defaults to `zh-CN` (catalog); the mapping wrote it as the comment's date, so every delivered comment of the live file said «11个月前» in «تاريخ ووقت التعليق». A source that dates a comment only by a relative text now gives no date (the cell is empty); a request with `language_code: "en"` would give «11 months ago», which `approxInstantOfRelative` already reads, but it was not asked on a real call and is not done (a paid test on the test account is needed). **Not seen:** a reply thread of more than one page (the next-page token of a page that has more replies is the catalog's), and `language_code` other than the default. **What this changes for customers:** both cards are verified, so every account, not only a test account, gets the comments tool with the reply tokens and the replies step (a customer's plan said «not included» for the replies before). Changed existing test lines (marked CHANGED): `tests/platform-cards.test.ts`, `tests/platform-youtube.test.ts`.

**Why `youtube.youtube.video.detail` failed in planning** (the stored call 5 and its response, `raw/call_0005.json`): HTTP 404 with the body «no youtube credential in this org — youtube.youtube.video.detail is a marketplace endpoint; connect one: `treg connections connect --provider youtube`, or register the tool yourself ... another provider serves youtube.video.detail: `crawl4ai.youtube.video.detail` ~0.0009 USD/call, `tikhub.x.youtube-web-v2-get-video-info` ~0.001, `tikhub.x.youtube-web-v2-get-video-info-v2` ~0.001 (callable now on treg's key)». The call was not billed (0.000000). The tool is free (0 USD) and carries `statistics.commentCount`, but our provider account has no YouTube connection (decision 7.7). Of the three named alternatives, the catalog lists a comment counter for none (`crawl4ai` gives title, channel, length, views, likes, publish date; the two `tikhub` ones list no fields): not verified, not switched. The plan was made without the counter and the promise stayed open, as designed.

**2. Instagram: which field made the 0.** On the live pages the comments tool answers `replies: null` on all 57 comment rows (calls 3 to 6: 16 + 11 + 17 + 13); the delivered rows hold `replies_count: ""` on all 30 comments; the file REBUILT from those real rows with the code of #357 (before #358) has an EMPTY «عدد الردود» on every comment (checked in a worktree of `6197074`); 17 of the 30 comments have 0 likes, and those are the zeros in «الإعجابات». **No 0 was in a counter field of the source or of the stored rows; the earlier hypothesis of #358 (a source value of 0 or `[]`) was not what the live page did.** The only 0 made from the missing counter was `Number(null)` in the planning sentence «٠ ردًا بحسب عدّادات ٢٧ تعليقًا», and #358 covers that and writes «غير متاح» in every comment's cell (test on the real rows: `tests/round12-instagram-live.test.ts`: all 30 say «غير متاح», the 17 zero likes stay 0, 9 replies).

**3. Facebook period.** On the real pages of `msn_9d196025e7929324983a` the fill search (`anyapi.facebook.search_posts`, call 3) WAS sent `startDate 2026-08-03` and `endDate 2026-11-03` (the call log), as were both pages of the keyword search; the source answered 20 posts of which 2 are outside the period (2026-01-19, the 1,823-comment post that made the hold 26,653, and 2025-10-02). The source takes the dates as a request and does not keep to them, so **the period is now checked on the rows** (`rankedPoolOf`, `PERIOD_CHECKED_PLATFORMS` = Facebook, Instagram, YouTube; TikTok and X keep what they do): when the Ask has a period, a row whose date is outside the Cairo days (inclusive) or has no readable date is not in the pool, so no chosen parent, no spare and no exposure read can be outside it, whichever search read it; the card says «لم نختر أي منشور خارج الفترة المطلوبة (من ... إلى ...)؛ تركنا ... خارجها» and the «قرأنا ٢٩ منشورًا» count stays the posts read. The plan of the live request on the real pages: three posts inside the period, two spares inside, hold 604 (it was 26,653), no question of the cap. Tests: `tests/round12-facebook-period.test.ts` (red first: 5 failed).

**Tests.** New: `tests/round12-{youtube-verify,instagram-live,facebook-period}.test.ts`. Changed (marked CHANGED): `tests/fixtures-anonymised.test.ts`, `tests/platform-cards.test.ts`, `tests/platform-youtube.test.ts`.

**Not proven.** (1) The YouTube replies card on a thread of more than one page. (2) Whether `language_code: "en"` gives dates the code can read. (3) The engine was not replayed on the real YouTube pages (the pages and the stored delivered rows agree; no run). (4) The period rule for a YouTube video dated only by «7 years ago» (placed from the planning moment) is as coarse as that date.

## Live round 11 (3 Nov 2026 afternoon, after #357): five findings of Facebook, Instagram and YouTube (test account)

Base `main` = `6197074` (#357). One branch, one PR. **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched. Facebook, Instagram and YouTube changes are behind their per-platform flags (compiled plans only); TikTok and X: see the stated diff below.

**The three missions** (read-only SELECTs; Cairo = UTC+2): Facebook `msn_9d196025e7929324983a` (13:32 UTC, `plan_ready`, answer «fill from other pages», never approved), Instagram `msn_97cdab58dc82fc2039ed` (13:34, 41 delivered, 212 charged of 250 held), YouTube `msn_41a73a6b2c0b4a76ef0a` (13:38, 101 delivered, 97 charged of 242 held). No mission was executing (0 of 158 in `executing / planning / approved / queued`).

**Every SELECT of the diagnosis** (none wrote): S1 `information_schema.columns` of `missions`; S2 a count of missions in `executing / planning / approved / queued`; S3 `missions` created after 10:00 UTC (id, status, request, hold, charge, delivered); S4 the Facebook plan's `price`, `plan_public.price`, `compiled`, `notes_ar`, `answer_text`; S5 the Instagram and S6 the YouTube plan's `plan_public.price`, `compiled`, `notes_ar`, `summary_ar`, `files`; S7 the Instagram `plan_public` (type of `compiled`, stage keys, `expected_rows`) next to the stored plan's stage flags; S8 the Instagram `mission_events`; S9 `information_schema.columns` of `mission_calls`; S10 the Instagram `mission_calls`; S11 the Facebook `mission_events`; S12 the Facebook plan's stages (planned rows, flags, cost per row, endpoint); S13 the Facebook plan's pinned rows (counters, dates); S14 the `plan_public` stages of the Instagram and the YouTube mission; S15 the YouTube `mission_events` (plan, result, question, answer); S16 the stages' endpoints of the Instagram and YouTube plans; S17 the YouTube `mission_calls` grouped by endpoint and phase; S18 `mission_calls` of the Facebook comments and replies tools grouped by endpoint and phase (calls, rows, USD); S19 the last eight Facebook replies calls.

### Diagnosis, with the evidence

1. **Orders page: «٤١ من ٤٢» against «٤١ من ٤٩» (Instagram), and the same defect on YouTube (220 against 101).** Round 10's `promisedRowsOf` required `replies_open` on the stages it was given, but the stages the orders page reads are `plan_public->stages` (keys: key, title_ar, columns, deliver, parent_key, sample_rows, yield_share, expected_rows; S7), which never carry it, so the function always returned the job's own number. The test of round 10 used a stage shape that does not exist in the stored plan. The public plan stored AFTER the run already holds the judged numbers (Instagram replies 9, not the card's 2: S14: 2 + 38 + 9 = 49; YouTube 2 + 95 + 4 = 101). **Fix:** for a compiled plan the promised number is the sum of the delivered stages' `expected_rows` of the public plan (a plan that still waits holds the card's own numbers, whose sum is the job's number). Test on the two stored public plans: `tests/round11-orders-count.test.ts`, fixture `tests/fixtures/live/round11/stored-public-plans.json` (numbers only). One test of round 10 pinned the defect (marked CHANGED in `tests/round10-instagram.test.ts`).

2. **«عدد الردود» 0 on every Instagram comment.** Two defects reproduced on the real M16 pages: (a) `compilePlan` fed `Number(row.replies_count)` to the replies sizing, and `Number(null)` is 0, so 27 comments with NO counter were «counted» as 27 comments with no replies; this is where the card's «٠ ردًا بحسب عدّادات ٢٧ تعليقًا قرأناها» (stored `compiled.promises.comments_replies`: counted 0, counted_comments 27, S5) came from, and the same `Number(null)` made a parent with no counter look like one with no children. (b) The Instagram comments tools carry no reply counter (the real pages: `replies: null` on all 22 stored comment rows, M15 and M16), but the mapping read the source's `replies` field whatever it held: a source that fills it with 0 or `[]` gave a 0 for a comment whose replies were never counted. **The live raw page was not read (Storage): which of the two the live page did is not proven; both reach a 0 and both are closed.** **Fix:** `counterValueOf` (absent is unknown, never 0); a comments card with no reply counter (`card.counter === null`, Instagram's two) never reads the field; the promise line says «لا تعرض المنصة عدد الردود على كل تعليق قبل قراءته» when no counter was read; in the file of a compiled plan any counter column (likes, comments, shares, views, saves, followers, subscribers, replies_count) with no value reads «غير متاح» (a real 0 and a number stay as they are). **The diff on TikTok and X** (`tests/round11-tiktok-x-invariance.test.ts`, hashes taken on `6197074` from the stored real pages of M12, M14 and the X round-8 mission): the TikTok plan and its view are byte for byte identical; its file differs in 20 cells: «عدد الردود» of the 20 reply rows of the comments sheet (the replies tool carries no counter of a reply), empty before, «غير متاح» now. The X file differs in no counter cell.

3. **«علاقة النص بالموضوع» missing on the round-11 YouTube sheet.** The column was added only when some chosen item was not about the topic (and never for X): round 10's videos were not all about it, round 11's both were. Now the column is on the parent stage of every compiled plan whenever the Ask has a topic, with the value of each item. **The diff:** TikTok: none (M12's plan already had it); X: the tweets stage gains the column and each chosen tweet its value (plan, view and file; with the column taken out the three hashes are the old ones). Changed test: `tests/live-e-topic-second-query.test.ts` (it pinned «no column when all are about the topic»).

4. **Facebook: «السعر حتى ٩٬١٢٥» and «حجز مؤقت ٢٦٬٦٥٣».** The compiler reproduces the live plan to the point from the real M17 search page and a comments page with the live counters (`tests/helpers/facebookLiveShape.ts`): 4,879 rows at 4.750125 points, maximum charge 23,176, hold 26,653 (15% above, rounded up). **Row by row:** posts 3 (15 points); comments 1,896 = 15 + 1,823 + 58, the posts' own counters (9,006); replies counted from the reply counters of the 14 comments read at planning 22 (104); that is 1,921 rows = **9,125 = «السعر حتى»**; replies of the comments not read yet 2,958 = ceil(1,882 comments x the mean counter 22/14 = 1.571) (14,051); together 4,879 rows = 23,176; x 1.15 = **26,653**. The replies are 2,980 of the 4,879 rows and 3.576 of the 4.188 USD provider cost (85%): each reply row is priced as one call (0.0012 USD, `observedRowsPerPage` 1, see item 5). **Why it is so much higher than round 10's 2,020 / 5,129:** the fill answer put in the most commented posts of the pool; one of the three (id 122160334562832818, «مايسترو التكنولوجيا», dated 2026-01-19 in the stored row) has 1,823 comments, against 407 comments in round 10's three. **Not fixed, found while reading the pins:** that post is dated 19 January 2026, outside «آخر ٣ شهور» (3 Aug to 3 Nov): the period is a request to the source, and the fill chooses by comments among what was read; the file's «ضمن الفترة المطلوبة» mark is not set for Facebook. The exact stored text of the line the tester read: «لم نجد في ما قرأناه ما يكفي مما يطابق المصدر أو الدولة أو اللغة المطلوبة؛ ٢ من العناصر المختارة لا تطابق ذلك، وعليها علامة في الملف.» (the second line of `notes_ar`, S4; 2 of the 3 chosen carry the mark «المصدر المسمّى: غير مطابق»). **Fix:** `src/lib/agentMission/overLimit.ts` (read-only on `jobPointsCap.ts` and on the price: it only reads the plan's price) and `holdLimit.ts` (the tiny part the browser imports). A Facebook, Instagram or YouTube compiled plan whose hold is above the cap (25,000) or above the customer's balance (the shared `useBalance`; a balance still loading or failed is never read as zero) has NO approve button: the card says it is above the limit with the hold, says what the hold is made of (posts, comments, replies counted, replies not counted yet), and offers «جهّز نسخة أصغر», «عدّل الطلب» and «ألغِ المهمة». The smaller version is built and priced by code on the stored plan (`smallerVersionOf`: the most comments per post whose hold fits, by binary search, priced again by `priceMissionByCode`; the sentences say its numbers); the live plan gives 1,704 comments per post, hold 24,998. The action is `smaller` of `/api/missions/[id]` (`smallerVersionForCustomer`: only a `plan_ready` plan, recomputed on the server; the client can only name a lower hold than the cap). **Staged jobs above the cap belong to the money PR: not built.** TikTok, X and the old path are not touched (no `limit` key; test), and a view with no limit has no new key (the flags-off digests are unchanged).

5. **Facebook replies tools** (report only; nothing switched): see the table in the PR. In short: two replies tools, 8 real calls ever, all successful empty answers; no Facebook reply row was ever seen, so the «one row per call» that sizes 85% of the price is an assumption, not a measurement.

**Tests.** New: `tests/round11-{orders-count,counters,topic-mark,facebook-limit,tiktok-x-invariance}.test.ts`, helpers `tests/helpers/{round11Worlds,facebookLiveShape}.ts`, fixture `tests/fixtures/live/round11/stored-public-plans.json`. Changed existing test lines (marked CHANGED): `tests/round10-instagram.test.ts`, `tests/live-e-topic-second-query.test.ts`, `tests/platform-cards.test.ts`.

**Not tested / not proven:** (1) the three live missions' raw pages (Storage): the Instagram `replies` value of the live comment page and the Facebook live counters beyond the stored plan; to confirm and to commit the live pages as fixtures the owner attaches the admin ZIP of `msn_97cdab58dc82fc2039ed`, `msn_9d196025e7929324983a` and `msn_41a73a6b2c0b4a76ef0a`. (2) The card was rendered to static markup in tests, not in a browser, and the balance comparison was not run against a real balance. (3) The smaller version is a plan by code; the engine was not run on it (it sets `per_parent_rows`, which the engine honours for a compiled comments stage; no replay). (4) The replies rows per call of any Facebook replies tool.

## Live round 10 (3 Nov 2026 afternoon, after #356): seven findings of Facebook, Instagram and YouTube (test account)

Base `main` = `8fd37c6` (#356). One branch, one PR. **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched. Behind the per-platform flags (compiled path only); TikTok and X: the sha256 of the map code of every one of their cards is pinned in a test, and the Ask, the pool ranking and the cards of every stored TikTok and X replay are unchanged.

**The three missions** (read-only SELECTs; Cairo = UTC+2): Facebook `msn_f189151e625d45564d58` (14:05, `plan_ready`, never approved), Instagram `msn_fc40280193aab21b9201` (14:07, 43 delivered, 182 charged of 286 held), YouTube `msn_67cafa6747d70356d9cb` (14:14, 42 delivered, 156 charged of 200 held). No mission was executing at 14:59 Cairo.

**Every SELECT of the diagnosis** (none wrote): S1 `information_schema.columns` of `missions`; S2 `missions` by the request words (3 rows); S3 the Facebook plan's `notes_ar`, `compiled`, `price`, `plan_public.price`; S4 its stages (`jsonb_array_elements(plan->'stages')`: endpoint, planned rows, params, pins); S5 its `mission_calls`; S6 its pinned and spare rows; S7 `mission_calls` rows with `status='refused'` (reason); S8 to S10 the same three (plan, stages, calls) for Instagram; S11 to S13 the same three for YouTube; S14 the YouTube comments and replies stages' `sample_rows`, `parent_binding`, flags; S15 the YouTube `mission_events`; S16 a count of missions in `executing / planning / approved / queued`.

### Diagnosis, with the evidence

1. **Facebook: the named shortfall card did not fire.** The pool was 26 posts in 3 pages (calls: 7 + 5 rows of the keyword search, 20 of the second search, a test-account read). The card said «منها من صفحة «العربية»: ٤ ... منها من الصفحة ويتحدث نصه عن الموضوع: ٢». Three causes, all in code: (a) `pageMatchesSource` took any page whose name CONTAINS the word «العربية»: the chosen posts were «صباح العربية» (a programme's page) and a post of «المملكة العربية السعودية الميرة الذكاء الاصطناعي» (caption «Hey everyone», link `permalink.php`); (b) the shortfall counted the chosen posts that were the page's, whatever they were about (found 3 of 3 asked), but decision 7.5 and the customer's request are «posts ABOUT X from page Y», and only 2 of the page's posts talked about the topic; (c) the link of a `permalink.php?story_fbid=..&id=..` post lost its query (`link.split('?')[0]`), so that post's comments could never be read. Also found: its comments read was **refused** in planning with `repeat_sample:planning` (S7): the gateway allows two samples of the comments tool, so the third chosen post was never checked (exposure check limited to two reads by the protected `gateway.ts`; not changed). **Fix:** the page is matched by its NAME word for word (the Arabic words of its name, kind words and «ال» off; a Latin handle by the link's last part); the shortfall counts the page's posts that talk about the topic and are chosen; «only the ones found» keeps exactly those; the link keeps `story_fbid`, `id`, `fbid`, `set`, `v` of `permalink.php / story.php / photo.php / watch` and drops every tracking parameter (Facebook cards only). **A related defect the owner's flow would have hit (found while testing the question):** after the customer answers a question the planning runs again with a new gateway rebuilt from the recorded calls, and the two-samples rule (`repeat_sample`) refuses the pool read a second time, so the plan could not be made (`pool_refused`). `planByCompiler` now serves a read whose endpoint and inputs equal a recorded ok planning call from that call's saved page (`priorCalls`): no new call, no new cost.
   **The price «٢٬٠٢٠ نقطة» (not approved), row by row.** Unit price 4.729176 points a row = (provider 0.7848 + model 0.026 USD) / 943 rows x 10 (markup) x 550 (points per USD). Counted part: 3 posts (14.2) + 407 comments (1,924.7) + 17 replies the 13 comments read at planning say they have (80.4) = 427 rows x 4.729176 = 2,019.4, shown 2,020. The open part «حتى ٢٬٤٤٠»: 516 replies for the comments not read yet (2,440.1). Together the maximum charge 4,460 (943 rows, 4,459.5 rounded up); the hold 5,129 = 4,460 x 1.15. Where the money is: the replies tool answers about one row per call at 0.0012 USD, so the 533 replies rows are 0.64 of the 0.78 USD provider cost while they are 57% of the rows. Nothing is charged before approval, and only for what is delivered.

2. **Instagram: the most commented of the last 12 posts, not of a deep pool.** Not «the newest»: the two chosen (13:19 and 13:51 Cairo, counters 45 and 9) are the two most commented of the pool, but the pool was ONE page: one call, 12 rows (S9), because the second page was read only when the request had a period. The pool therefore covered only the last few hours of a news account (the page itself is in storage: the pins and spares in the plan are 11:19, 11:31, 11:44 and 11:51 UTC; the oldest of the 12 was not read here). **Fix:** with no period the listing's next page is read whenever the source has one (the gateway's two samples of a listing, untouched), by the card's own `next_max_id` when the gateway does not name the cursor: 24 posts; with a period, only while the first page's oldest post is still inside it. The card says before approval «اخترنا الأكثر تعليقًا من آخر N منشورًا، وهي المنشورات التي نُشرت من ... إلى ... بتوقيت القاهرة» with N and the period of the pool (not said when the customer asked for the newest). **The two numbers:** the orders page said «٤٣ من ٥٩» (the job's target = the card's total at approval, 2 + 54 + 3), the chat and the file «٤٣ من ٦٣» (the plan as judged at the end, the replies judged against the reply counters of the delivered comments: 2 + 54 + 7). The one promised number is now the judged total on every surface (`promisedRowsOf`, used by the orders page; the job row and the money are not touched).

3. **YouTube: the file was not honest about a gap.** The record: video 1 gave 40 comments, video 2 none, 0 replies; the card said 54 rows and the file said «لا قيود سُجّلت». Causes: (a) the OPEN comments stage was sized by the first pages the planning saw (20 + 20 = 40 rows) and the engine stops a stage at its planned rows, so video 1's two pages filled it and video 2 was never read (`stage_1_used 1`, no call for video 2 in S12); (b) the gap lines were written only for a parent whose row carries a counter, and YouTube has none, so no line existed; the free counter card failed in planning («no youtube credential in this org»). **Why video 2 was not swapped:** its first comments page was NOT empty (20 rows at planning), so the exposure check passed it; it got nothing because of (a), not because the source was empty. **Fix:** an open comments stage is sized per chosen video (`OPEN_COMMENTS_PER_PARENT` = 100: the hold's maximum and the per-video reading limit, stored as `per_parent_rows` and `children_cap_per_parent`), the card says «حتى ١٠٠ تعليق من كل عنصر مختار ...»; `openThreadGapLinesAr` writes a line per video in the file's «عدّاد المصدر وما سُلّم» and «حدود هذا التسليم»: comments delivered (against the counter when there is one, else it says the source shows none), a video that gave none (with the stage's recorded stop), the per-video limit reached, and the reply counters of the delivered comments against the replies delivered. A chosen video whose first page is empty is still swapped before approval (decision 7.3), and during execution by the stage's spares (#344).

4. **YouTube replies: none were read.** On this test account the catalog_only `tikhub.youtube.video.comments` card WAS used (the plan's comments stage endpoint; its rows carry `replies_count` and `reply_continuation_token`, S14) and the replies card `anyapi.youtube.comment_replies` was in the plan (S11), so the right tools were chosen. Cause: the compiled comments stage did not declare `carry_keys`, so the engine's ingest dropped each comment's reply token from the stored row, the replies stage found no `continuationToken` to ask with, `usable` was empty and it broke silently: no call (S12 shows none), `stage_3_rows 0`. The same defect hid Facebook's `feedback_id` and `expansion_token`. **Fix:** the compiler sets `carry_keys` from the comments card's tokens whenever it builds a replies stage (TikTok and X cards have no tokens: unchanged). On another account the card keeps saying before approval «لا توجد بعد أداة موثّقة لقراءة الردود على هذه المنصة، فلن تُضاف هذه الخطوة» (the verified comments tool has no reply token; tested).

5. **YouTube topic mark.** `topicTierOf` required every word of «نهائي السوبر المصري الأهلي والزمالك» in the text, and «والزمالك» (the «و» glued) matched nothing. Now the key tokens: each word without «ال» and a glued «و»; a topic of up to three words is matched as before (every word); a longer one needs three fifths of its tokens (5 words: 3). The two live titles are about the topic (3 of 5 and 4 of 5). Same rule on every platform. **The exact diff on the TikTok and X fixtures is empty:** `tests/round10-topic-invariance.test.ts` compares the old rule (copied) with the new one over every string of the stored TikTok and X pages and made-up captions, for six topics of one to three words: not one tier differs. The only behavioural change is a topic of four words or more, or a «و» glued to a name (Facebook, Instagram and YouTube cases).

6. **YouTube «القنوات الرسمية» read only Al Ahly SC.** Both clubs' channels WERE read (S12: two channel searches, then two in-channel searches of 30 rows, `UCA86...` and `UCeFU...`); the pool had both. The two chosen videos (and both spares) were from the first channel because the ranking ties (no counter, no topic tier) kept the source's order. **Fix:** (a) the channels take turns inside each level of the topic relation (`interleaveByChannel`: choosing, never filtering); (b) the F1 understand step: a small structured request to the review model (`agent/src/runMission.ts`, `understand`, cost from the API's usage added to the mission's model cost, never a charge) names the organisations whose official channels are meant (both clubs, and the league only if the request names it); `checkChannelsAgainstWords` keeps a name only when every word of it is in the customer's own words, a Latin or invented name is dropped, nothing usable leaves the names read from the topic; up to three channels are read (the third only when named); (c) the card lists the channels read (their Arabic names) and says code cannot check that they are the official ones.

7. **Decision 7 (a recurring event).** Before any read, for Facebook, Instagram and YouTube (not TikTok or X), a topic with a final, a cup, a championship, a league, a season, an election, a super cup, a world cup, an olympiad, a summit, a festival, a derby, a round or a conference, and no year, no period and no «هذا العام / الماضي» in the words, shows «تقصد أنهي مرة؟» with three answers: «آخر مرة (الافتراضي)», «كل المرات», «حدد سنة» (the last asks for the year in words; a typed year makes that calendar year the period). «آخر مرة»: among the rows that talk about the topic, those published within 300 days of the newest come first, then the newer before the older (ranking only); a source that dates a video only by «7 years ago» is placed in time from the planning moment (`approxInstantOfRelative`). The chosen occurrence is kept in the mission's run note (`RunState.occurrence`) because the answer of a later question (the Facebook page question) replaces `answer_text`. Nothing is read or priced before the answer.

**Changed existing test lines (marked CHANGED):** `tests/platform-youtube.test.ts` (its helper answers the new question with «كل المرات»), `tests/platform-facebook.test.ts` (three tests give the one deliverable post a caption about the topic: what is counted is posts about the topic), `tests/platform-instagram.test.ts` (the pool is two reads; the second page is not served by any stored page).

**Not tested / not proven:** (1) the three live missions' own raw pages were not read (Storage): the tests use the real M17, M16, M15 and M2 pages (same endpoints, same shapes), with the structure the live cards reported put back (author names, captions: the anonymiser replaced them); the second Instagram page is M15's real page 1 read at another time, declared in the test; the tikhub comments and anyapi replies pages of YouTube are written in the shape of the plan's sample rows (no real page of either is stored). To commit the live pages as fixtures, the owner attaches the admin ZIP of `msn_f189151e625d45564d58`, `msn_fc40280193aab21b9201`, `msn_67cafa6747d70356d9cb`. (2) The understand step was tested with a fake model call; the real request and its 200-token answer were not made. (3) What the replies tool answers for a real YouTube token. (4) The orders page change is display only and was not rendered in a browser. (5) The Facebook comments read of the third chosen post is still limited by the gateway's two samples of the tool.

- **Cards** (`src/lib/agentMission/foundation/toolCards/{facebook,instagram,youtube}.ts`, data): 18 cards. **Verified on real stored pages (10):** Facebook keyword search with dates (M17 calls 4 and 5 and the seven other search missions), Facebook page listing (M17, M19), Facebook comments (scrapecreators); Instagram account listing (scrapecreators, paged by `next_max_id`: every real page shows it with `more_available`), Instagram comments (both), Instagram replies (anyapi; one real page has a reply row, twelve are successful `not found` answers); YouTube channel search, search inside a channel, comments (crawl4ai). **`catalog_only` (8, test accounts only, inside the 0.20 USD a mission):** Facebook second keyword search, Facebook `post_comments`, both Facebook replies tools (8 of 8 real reads were successful empty answers: no reply row was ever seen), Instagram `anyapi` account listing (0 calls ever), YouTube free detail card (counter; needs YouTube connected on our provider account, decision 7.7), YouTube `tikhub` comments (the one with a reply counter and a reply token; no real page stored), YouTube `anyapi` replies.
- **Facebook.** Keyword + the two Cairo days of the request (`2026-08-02` to `2026-11-02` for «آخر ٣ شهور» on 2 Nov) through the search; the page listing is chosen only when the Ask has no topic and no period and the page is given by its link or a Latin handle. The named page is CHOSEN among the pool rows by its author (never filtered). The pool is the two search pages the protected two-samples rule allows (decision 7.6: `GATEWAY_MAX_SAMPLES_PER_SOURCE = 2`, untouched), and the card says how many posts were read, how many are the page's and how many talk about the topic. A test account also reads the second keyword search once. **Decision 7.5:** fewer deliverable posts from the named page than asked gives a question card before approval (only the ones found, fill from other pages with the mark «المصدر المسمّى: غير مطابق», or cancel).
- **Instagram.** The account by handle (an Arabic name cannot be written in `handle`: the plan is not made and the reason is honest). 12 posts a page; the two most commented of the pool; comments counter from the listing; the replies promise is open (every real comment row has a null reply counter).
- **YouTube.** The official channels are looked up by name first (names are read from the topic's «X و Y» tail, or given by the edit/Stage 2: `AskEdits.channels`), each of the first channels is read INSIDE with the topic (the gateway allows two samples of that search), then the comments. A request whose channels cannot be read is not planned (never a plain search with no channel input: that is why M18 and M20 once took a video of another channel).
- **Honest limits (NOT VERIFIED):** which channel is official is not decidable by code (the card says how many channels it read); the YouTube counter promise needs the detail card to answer on a live run; Facebook reply rows and the second Facebook search have never answered with rows; the `max_pages` of a catalog_only stage limits a stream, as for X.

The snapshot file `data/treg/treg_catalog_sellable.json` is the owner's attached file, pinned by size and sha256 (`tests/treg-catalog-data.test.ts`, `data_policy.json`) and is NOT edited: a first version edited it and those two guards failed, so the edit was removed and the guards kept. ## Live round 7 of the 3 Nov 2026 (after #351): the second topic query and its question, the X replies gap line (2026-11-03)

Base `main` = `0fcec7d` (#351). One branch, one PR. **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched. Round-7 missions (test account): TikTok `msn_4e68812d396bd5db0a01`, X `msn_9fa5ba384c05a25247e1`.

- **Why round 7 and `msn_e32cd467fe6bbdfa943e` got different pools** (read-only SELECTs on `mission_calls.params_digest` and the plans' `pinned_rows`/`spare_rows`). All three TikTok missions made the SAME search call: keyword «الذكاء الاصطناعي», region EG, sort_type 1, count 20. The mission of 07:11 and the round-7 mission (08:30) chose the same four videos (two chosen, two spare); `msn_e32cd467` (21:58 the night before) has four others, none in common. The source returned 20 rows then and 10 rows now for the identical request. So the difference is what the source answered at another time, not our query, sort, region or page.
- **Item 1** (`agent/src/compiled.ts`, `foundation/topicChoice.ts`, `compilePlan.ts`, `runMission.ts`). TikTok only, «عن X» only (not «معمول بـ X»). When no video of the first page has its own text about the topic, the one more read the gateway allows for the source (`GATEWAY_MAX_SAMPLES_PER_SOURCE = 2`, untouched) asks the topic with the words of talking about it (`aboutQueryOf`: «X شرح وأخبار»), and the union of both pages is ranked by the same rule. If the union still has none, `planByCompiler` returns `kind: "question"` and `runMission` stores a `needs_input` card BEFORE approval with three answers: continue with what was found (marked), change my request, cancel. «Change» and «cancel» end the mission as cancelled before anything is read (nothing is held); «continue» plans from ONE pool read as before, the card says honestly that no video is about the topic, and the videos sheet gets the mark column «علاقة النص بالموضوع» (`topic_relation`: يتحدث نصه عن الموضوع / يذكر الموضوع في الوسوم فقط / لا يذكر الموضوع) whenever some chosen video is not about it. Any other text the customer types is read as an amendment of the request. Nothing is filtered; the question only decides whether the plan is made from what was found.
- **Item 3** (`threadCounters.ts`). The file's «حدود هذا التسليم» now says the replies gap per TWEET (the reply counter of the delivered tweet against the replies delivered under it; a tweet with no counter and no reply is not listed; more than the counter is told in the counter line, not called a gap). Compiled plans only. Real numbers (SELECTs): counters 23, 5, 3, 1, 1 (33) against replies delivered 16, 4, 1, 2, 2 (25).
- **Item 2 DONE: the card is NOT marked verified** (the owner's ZIP of the three missions was read from the scratch folder; 8 anonymised files committed under `tests/fixtures/live/missions/msn_9fa5ba384c05a25247e1` and `msn_4e68812d396bd5db0a01`; `tests/live-e-x-replies-real.test.ts`). Verified on the five real replies pages: the list path `output.data.items`, the reply ids, the fields, and the parent link (`conversationId` = the tweet asked, on all 25 rows). NOT as the card assumed: `output.data.nextCursor` is present and NULL on every page (the 16-row page included) and the response's own hint says its source cannot return a nextCursor, so the result may be incomplete and there is no way to continue (it names a `requireFields: ["nextCursor"]` re-run that is not an input of our catalog entry and may cost more per request). So the tweet of counter 23 gave 16 with NO token to follow: not an engine bug. Two tweets gave more rows than their counter (1 → 2): the counter counts direct replies, the page is the whole conversation (one row had a reply of its own). The three short ones are 5 → 4, 23 → 16, 3 → 1. The card stays `catalog_only`. A fix needs the owner: a catalog input for `requireFields` (data file, price unknown) and a live run to see its cost; the 0.10 USD cap is sized from the catalog price.

**Real-caption check (owner's ZIP, scratch only):** on the real round-7 page exactly ONE of 10 videos has the topic in its caption body, and it is a Saudi video, so the first version of the «pool has an about video» check counted it and would have asked nothing (fixed: `isAboutAndEligible`, the video must also meet the named source, country and language; red first on the real-structure page). With the real captions the plan would choose 7650084504324902176 and 7692547380482346263 (the same two as live) after the second search and the question; on the real page of `msn_e32cd467fe6bbdfa943e` (4 eligible about videos) it chooses 7604534273298812180 and 7691663269223795975, the two that run chose.

**NOT PROVEN.** (1) The anonymiser replaces captions, so the item-1 tests use the real anonymised round-7 and M12 search pages (ids, counters, countries, languages) with MADE-UP captions: what the source answers to the SECOND query is unknown; the next live run shows it. (2) The item-3 numbers are real (SELECTs), the ids and texts are the anonymised M14 page's, not the live mission's own rows. (3) The question card was tested through `planByCompiler` and the compiled-planning run, not on a live mission.

Base `main` = `2f3125d` (#350). One branch, one PR. **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or settlement line is touched. - **D1 topic relation** (`foundation/pool.ts`, `compilePlan.ts`). Both chosen videos were AI-made cartoon stories with the topic only in hashtags (#ai #ذكاء_اصطناعي). `factsOf` counted the topic words anywhere in the caption. Now `topicTierOf`: 2 = the words are in the caption BODY (hashtags taken out), 1 = only inside hashtags (the tool, not the subject), 0 = absent; the pool ranks by that tier (still after the named source and country/language, before the counter). «معمول بـ X» counts a hashtag match as 2. The plan card says so before approval when the pool read has no video that talks about the topic, or how many chosen ones are below it. Ranking only, nothing is dropped.

The site and the notice told the judged replies against their counters (`٦٣ من ٦٦`); the file added the replies delivered (`٦٣ من ٦٣`). `buildMissionFiles(..., { repliesJudged })` now uses the same rule as the notice; without the option the sentence is byte for byte what it was.
- **D4 the gap line** (`threadCounters.ts`). A compiled plan that asked a number of comments per video says, per video: comments asked against delivered, and the reply counters of the DELIVERED comments against the replies delivered. The video's whole counter is never set against a per-video limit. Old-path plans keep their lines.
- **D5 X** (`compilePlan.ts`, `pool.ts`, `toolCards/x.ts`, `engine/run.ts`, `workbook.ts`, `repliesCard.ts`). Tweets asked WITH replies are requested as originals (`-filter:replies`); a reply that still comes ranks below the originals (11 of the 20 tweets of the stored M14 delivery were replies). The replies are a child stage (`replies_of: tweets`) read by a **`catalog_only`** card, `anyapi.x.post.comments` (catalog entry only), built **only for a test account** (`planByCompiler({ catalogOnly })`, from `redesign.isTest`), with `max_pages` = floor(0.10 / price) = 200 so the catalog_only calls of a mission cost at most 0.10 USD (the engine honours `stage.max_pages`). Other accounts keep the «لم تُضَف ... أداة موثّقة» line. A tweet whose own counter says 0 is not read. The replies of a top-level item are their own sheet (not threaded into the tweets sheet).

## Live findings A, B, C of the 3 Nov 2026 batch (after #349): X on the old path, the Cairo days, the TikTok card (2026-11-03)

Base `main` = `75f21de` (#349). One branch, one PR for B and C. **No SQL write, no migration, no flag change, no paid provider call.** No money, ledger, billing, `gateway.ts`, `price.ts`, `jobPointsCap.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` or `finalize.ts` file is touched. (`docs/FOUNDATION-PLAN.md`, named by the session prompt, is not in the repository; `docs/REDESIGN-PLAN.md` is the plan that exists.)

**A (X ran on the old path): the switch, and also a named fallback.** `agent_settings.redesign_flags` was updated 2026-11-03 06:19:38 UTC; the audit rows (`admin_audit_log`) say `x: inherit → test` at 06:19:22 and `tiktok: inherit → test` at 2026-11-02 21:57:05. The X mission `msn_24fd8dc0470a2c990cc0` was created at 06:05:32, so X had NO key of its own: the compiled planner (`compiledPlatformActive` needs the platform's own key) did not run. Its recorded run event is `pipe_new: 0, fallback_stage: 2`: the type flags were `test`, so the old planner's plan was run by the old path, and stage 2 (the replies) had no recipe, so the model carried the mission out. Both are true; nothing was changed for A.

**B (the date).** The stored recipe held the right Cairo moments (`since:2026-09-30_21:00:00_UTC until:2026-10-31_22:00:00_UTC`), but the calls that ran (`mission_calls`, calls 6 to 8) carried the model's own `since:2026-10-01 until:2026-10-31` (day only): the source read `until:2026-10-31` as the whole UTC day, so tweet 2116654976390795707 (2026-10-31 22:14:06 UTC = 2026-11-01 00:14 Cairo, by its snowflake id) came back and the old period filter (UTC days, `…T23:59:59Z`) accepted it; `since:2026-10-01` also missed the first three hours of 1 October in Cairo. #341/S10 fixed this only in `buildToolInput` and the walk windows, which run under a flag for a code-built stage. The methodology printed the UTC date of the stamps («من ٣٠ سبتمبر ٢٠٢٦»). Fix (old path, own tests in `tests/live-b-x-period.test.ts`): `alignQueryToCairoPeriod` (`dateWindow.ts`) writes the Cairo moments of the plan's period into a bare-day query, used for the engine's first read and for the model's own `treg_call` in the execution phase (`agent/src/periodRequest.ts`; a search for the replies to an account or a conversation is not a search of the period and is left alone); the ingest period filter reads Cairo days (`cairoDays: true`); `plainSearchAr` reads a stamp as its Cairo day. The compiled path was already right: Cairo days inclusive in the search inputs and `markVerdict` gives «ضمن الفترة المطلوبة» = لا outside (a test now proves both).

**C (the TikTok card, `msn_b9eb261694fa611013d4`, plan_ready, untouched).** The card's items (`stagesView`) leave out the open replies stage, but the price (`priceMissionByCode`) counts its rows: 2 + 200 + 1,875 = 2,077 rows x 4.590925 = 9,536 (hold 10,966 = 1.15 x). 1,875 = ceil(200 x 375 / 40): the mean reply counter of the 40 comments read at planning applied to all 200 chosen, with a flat cap of 50 per comment. The engine then read each comment for at most that cap and stopped the stage at the estimate (a comment with 120 replies gave 50). Fix (compiled plans only, `tests/live-c-tiktok.test.ts`): `repliesSizing` counts the comments read at planning by their REAL counters and sizes only the rest by the capped mean (the cap sizes the hold, never a delivery); the card counts the counted replies in its items, shows «السعر حتى» for those items and a separate «ردود لم تُعدّ بعد (حتى)» line (rows and price) so the two add up to the unchanged maximum and hold; with no number per comment the replies stage is `replies_read_all` and the engine reads every reply of every comment that has replies (`replies_read_all` + `readsAllReplies`), the stage being bounded while it runs only by the job's own delivered-rows limit (`withPromiseCaps`), the charge by the unchanged reserve rule. A number per comment from the customer is still a number. Plans compiled BEFORE this PR (stored without these fields) keep their old card and the old cap: re-plan to see the new behaviour.

**NOT PROVEN.** The raw first comment pages of `msn_b9eb261694fa611013d4` and the raw pages of `msn_24fd8dc0470a2c990cc0` are in storage (not read by this session): the tests use the stored plan records (read by SELECT, anonymised) with the real M12 page shapes; no stored page has a reply counter above 5, so the comment with more than 50 replies is the real page with one counter set. A live run of the compiled X path and a live read of a comment with more than 50 replies were not made.

## ROOT CAUSE and the ONE fix (2026-11-02, after #344): the request contract, the final gate, per-platform flags

Read `docs/ROOT-CAUSE.md` first: the 20 live missions of the test account, the shared causes with file:line, and the design. In one paragraph: nothing checked the finished plan against what the customer asked (a stage was demanded only by a NUMBER, so «مع كل التعليقات والردود» demanded nothing: the 19:00 YouTube plan had neither comments nor replies and was approved); the plan is rewritten by ~10 ordered passes with no gate after the last; the new pipeline falls back to the old one silently; and the tests described invented worlds. Fix: `src/lib/agentMission/requestContract.ts` (built once from the words; final gate in `propose_plan`), per-platform flags (`tiktok`, `x`, `instagram`, `facebook`, `youtube`, same `redesign_flags` value, no key = follows the type, admin rows, same audit), the recorded `fallback_stage`, and `tests/replay-live-missions.test.ts` (the 20 stored missions; add every new live mission to `tests/fixtures/live/replay-missions.json` BEFORE writing its fix). No SQL, no money file, no flag switched.

## Golden run round 2 (G1, G4): the place filter, the limits text, the edit titles, ten replies per comment (2026-11-01, after #327)

Base `main` = `f044b3165a5ba19191c0e51fa91177a61ffb1afe` (#327). One branch, one commit per item (4), one PR. **No SQL, no migration, no Edge Function.** `price.ts`, `agent/src/gateway.ts`, `src/functions/**`, `supabase/**` are untouched; `finalize.ts` has TEXT changes only (the set of reasons the truthful sentence is said for), no settlement line. Caps unchanged (job 25,000 points; provider daily 0.80 USD).

**Live missions (TEST account, read-only SELECTs; Cairo = UTC+2).** G1 `msn_9dc8f2db86bc5cf4959b` (17:33): 3 of 15, charged 266. G4 `msn_32fc60fb7bab0c8c7869` (17:38): 198 delivered (5 videos, 9 comments, 184 replies), charged 152.

**1. Root cause of G1 (NOT PR #326).** The stored plan carries one place filter, written by the planner: `{kind: "place", field: "city", values: ["EG"]}`, with no `places` and no `fields` (the plan of the 15:12 run, `msn_ecca2a0d074fef9f4456`, had NO place filter, so it never met this). `placesInText("EG")` finds nothing (a two-letter name is not matched in a text, `places.ts:129`) and «مصرية» is a nationality, not a place, so the plan check (`plan.ts`, place block) never named the places, and the engine fell to the plain branch of `filterVerdict` (`filters.ts`, the no-`places` case): the CITY cell («al qahirah», «giza») against the text «eg». Progress events: 50, 100 checked with 0 accepted, 242 checked with 2 accepted and 240 `excluded`. #326 F4 (`countryOfRequestText`) is used only by the company-phone helper (`engine/contacts.ts:216`) and is not a row filter; a test keeps it that way (the request's nationality word never creates a filter). Fix: `placeKeysOfValues` (ISO code, name, city, nationality), `resolveFilters` names the places from the values (Arabic labels on the card, `by_source` then applies when the source was asked for the country), `filterVerdict` reads every place field of the item and resolves a stored values-only filter at evaluation time. Replay with the live shape (a city in Latin letters, an empty country, free text «Faiyum Egypt», «egypt», no location field with the source asked for EG, one German company): 5 of 5 pass, the German one is the only row left out. Permanent test `golden-r2-g1-location.test.ts`.
**2. Limits text.** `stageTruthBodyAr` (the recorded counts) said something only for people's row rules. It now also fires for every filter of the plan (place, period, account, media, replies, reposts, government) and `not_at_company`, with the stage's own noun: «وُجد ٢٤٢ شركة، ولم يُسلَّم ٢٤٠: ٢٤٠ من مكان غير الذي حددته». The language filter and the topic check keep their own true sentences. Test `golden-r2-limits-truth.test.ts`.
**3. Edit titles.** `planEdit.ts` moved a count only by digit inside the summary/how/assumption sentences and not at all in the titles; with 5 videos x 2 comments the planned rows were 10 too, which kept the stale sentence alive. `restateNounCount` moves a count standing in front of THE STAGE'S OWN noun (digits or spelled) to the new count in the right Arabic form («أعلى تعليقين»), in the stage title, the headline, the explanations and the assumptions; a total-count edit moves a title that carries the stage's count; the replies stage's title is rebuilt from its comments'. Test `golden-r2-g4-edit-titles.test.ts`.
**4. Replies default 10 (owner decision), editable up to the cap of 50.** `AGENT_REPLIES_PER_COMMENT_DEFAULT` 10; the editor has «عدد الردود لكل تعليق» (while the replies are on), the edit is `replies_per_comment` (whole number 1 to 50); the stage stores the sampled counters and the call price so the plan is re-priced by the same rule (`repliesEstimateFor`). **G4 price, fixture with the live shape (expected cost, not a live run):** 50 per comment 2,555 rows, hold 1,607, max charge 1,398 (live plan: 2,555 rows, hold 1,605, max 1,396); 10 per comment 555 rows, hold 469, max charge 408; with the customer's 10 -> 2 comments per video as well: 228 rows, hold 200 (the minimum). A call gives a page of ten replies, so reading 3 or 10 costs one call a comment: lowering below ten lowers the rows, not the cost. Test `golden-r2-g4-replies-default.test.ts`.

**5. Why G4 planning took 4-5 minutes (report only; targets of #326: companies at most 90 s, none above 240 s, at most 15,000 output tokens).** `missions.created_at` 15:38:27 to the `plan` event 15:43:02 UTC = **275 s** (4 min 35 s). Two planning runs: (a) 15:38:32 to 15:40:15 (105 s): 3 provider calls (crawl4ai videos 8.8 s, anyapi videos 3.8 s, comments 1.0 s), model output recorded 409 tokens, cache write 108,131 tokens, 0.0275 USD, and the worker's memory guard stopped it (`mem_stopped` 1, `mem_peak_tree_mb` 580 = `AGENT_MEMORY_HARD_LIMIT_MB`); the mission was requeued («تأجّلت مهمتك قليلًا بسبب ضغط على الخدمة»): **105 s and 0.0275 USD of model cost were thrown away**; (b) 15:40:17 to 15:43:02 (165 s): third sample at 15:40:52, then the model wrote for about 130 s: **36,577 output tokens (2.4x the 15,000 target)**, 852,924 cache-read tokens, 0.0341 USD. Total planning model cost 0.0615 USD (3x the 0.0201 of G1). What the model wrote: the stored plan is only 10,317 characters (videos 3,488, comments 2,321, the replies stage 3,527 of which 2,388 is code-written mapping), so roughly 2,000 tokens of the 36,577 are in the plan; the rest was tool-call arguments and reasoning across the multi-turn loop (the per-turn tokens are not stored: NOT PROVEN how they split). The video stage still carries 1,381 characters of sample rows although the prompt says to omit them. Provider calls are about 14 s of the 275 s: not the cause. **Both targets missed (275 s total, 165 s for the run that finished; 36,577 tokens); the single biggest loss was the memory stop (105 s), and the output tokens are what is left to cut.** Not changed in this PR (report only).

**Tests.** Base `f044b31`: 655 files (5 skipped) / 8,087 passed, 57 skipped, 0 failed. Changed existing test lines: `tests/helpers/g4RepliesWorld.ts` only (the replays about whole threads raise the number per comment to 50 in the plan editor before approving; the helper gained a `beforeApproval`; no assertion line changed). Rollback: `git revert -m 1 <merge commit>`.

**NOT PROVEN.** The live fill of the source's country on the 242 raw rows (storage, not readable by SQL); that the planner will write the same filter again (model-written); the real per-turn split of the 36,577 planning tokens; why the planning step reached 580 MB; the live price at ten replies per comment (a fixture, not a run); the real page size of `anyapi.tiktok.comment_replies` (live pages gave 20 rows).
